### Defaults used (T-051, round 2)

71. **New, a link's code (round 2; action-layer note d):** a secure link's short code is derived from its id by a keyed hash (HMAC-SHA-256 under a Workers secret, named in config and never stored: BP rule 7), cut to the short code's length, so the code is never kept: the commit stores only its sha256 (K-30 secure_links.code_sha256), and the slot filler (C-088) derives it again when finalize fills the link slot, in the same turn after its commit (K-05 writePlan) or in a later clock turn (default 72). It never enters a span, a trace, the event log or an error (v1 default 30). K-30 default 12 holds: a later message that needs a link gets a new one.
72. **New, a page's message to its reader (round 2, ruling 2e; runtime note b):** a page's commit writes no outbox row (v1.1 open gap 43), so a call made from a page whose step tells someone (the pricing page's or the portal's send, see first, decline) arms one K-04 clock row of kind step, due at once, on the reader's handle (the customer's mobile's when its chat is open, else their email's), whose clock turn writes the row, with its link filled at finalize (default 71): v1.1 default 56's and v1.2 default 66's way. A reader with neither an open chat nor an email (someone who came through their own AI) gets nothing armed: the link waits for their AI or page to read it (open gap 59).
73. **New, a teen's parent link (round 2, ruling 2a):** A4-96 and B4-108's link 'for a parent ... The parent books on the link, as their own booking' is the business's booking page (FS screen 1) for the service matched, naming no person and no booking, so it keeps no row: K-30 secure_links needs a person and a booking, and nothing about the teen is kept (RULES 4.7). Its host and path are config values (Q-51; the master's sample 'rosfield.com/c/teen'). It goes to the teen's own AI or page in url, and in a turn as the reply row in the teen's own thread only; Rosie never texts the parent.
74. **New, A4-80's checks (round 2, ruling 5):** the categories are FS screen 2's; who may book and the ages come from the plug-in, and with the plug-in silent an adult books (A4-80): for themself; for another adult, who gives their own yes later (A4-26); for an adult acted for only where the plug-in lets someone book for that adult, with the papers on the page (A4-64); for a child only as a parent or guardian; and a booker under 18 books for no one else (bookerNotAllowed). B4-100's 'only the person served, or someone with legal papers' ends anyone else there. A booker under 18 for themself is a teen of 13 to 17 or under 13, as decide reads it from their words, never by asking an exact age; an unclear age counts as under 18 and takes the parent's path (teenParentLink where a parent must book), never a private teen booking.
75. **New, the place pick (round 2, ruling 6; v1.1 open gap 27 closed):** `matchService` answers every active way of the service in both flows, each with its price now (K-25 service_money.flow, place; the resolver's set price for no named time or person, default 65). A way picked of the other flow than the request's moves the request there in the same commit: K-04 bookings.flow, written when the request opens and copied in once at booking, is set while the head is at stage entry, compared and set on the head's version, and the walk goes on in that flow's step graph (A4-02, B4-02, A4-15's 'Wants the other place'). v1.1 open gap 27's default (a request with no service yet opens in flow A) stands until the pick.
76. **New, journey 4's answer fields (round 2: AI notes a and b):** as v1.1 open gap 49 did for journey 3, decide's fields for journey 4's answers are named by this contract's own field names until a K-01 minor version lists them as answer fields: `matchService.request.words.asTyped` and `place.serviceMoneyId` (A4-81, A4-01), A4-80's `for`, `bookerAgeBand`, `under13`, `parentOrGuardian` and `name` (which code turns into `setWhoFor`'s outcome), `setReachWay` `way` and `contacts.mobile`, `.email` (A4-82), `addCustomer.name` (A4-23), `checkPlace.check.text` (B4-77), `showTimes.filter` (A4-16, A4-17, A4-83), the pick (A4-15, B4-16: decide's choice 'Picks a shown time' with the card's option id as its pick field, the card's times reaching decide as a fact: the times card's open question's options, K-04 open_questions.options) and the owner's changed price (`quoteFromPhotos` `newOptions`: A4-10, B4-11).
77. **New, addCustomer's way and band (round 2, ruling 7):** the request carries the reach card's way and the booker's own A4-80 band, built by the server side from where default 57 kept them (in a turn the answered options of A4-80's and A4-82's open questions; on the booking page the page server's session, K-21; for an AI the Agent API's token session, K-20), never from the browser's or the AI's words, and checked against what `setWhoFor` and `setReachWay` answered. No way their contacts imply and no left-out band ever stands in.
78. **New, the 4-hour pricing clock (round 2: runtime note c):** askPhotos arms one K-04 clock row of kind step, due 4 hours on (A4-08: 'If nobody prices it in 4 hours, Sam is asked to'; the master names no text hours here), on the owner's handle, whose clock turn writes the owner's ask and the customer's 'with the business' notice (A4-08, B4-09); the price stage cancels it in its commit. Round 3 (action-layer note b): its id is fixed by rule, `clock-<quote id>-pricing` (as `j4-ask-photos-agent.json` and `j4-quote-price-page.json` write it), which code builds from the quote's id and never parses (CQ-09), as K-05 builds `<reservation id>/saved-time`, so the price stage names it with no search. Its commit cancels it by that id and its version (K-05 v1 clockCancel takes both) while it is still waiting or armed; a row that already fired (the owner was asked) stays fired and is not cancelled. Reading that row by its id, with its state and version, is a K-05 read journey 4 needs (open gap 68).
79. **New, see first's times link (round 2, ruling 2b):** A4-94 and B4-101's 'See times' link is a K-30 secure_links row of purpose quote for the quote at see_first, whose page offers the visit to be priced there and its times ('booked like any visit'; 'Only the visit's own price is promised'), sent to the customer in the owner's turn or, from the portal, through default 72. Round 3 (action-layer note d; runtime note f): the estimate visit is booked on the service's visit-path way in the request's flow (K-25 v1 service_money with path visit|<before>|<after>; `showTimes` and `saveTime` name that `serviceMoneyId`, with `quoteOptionId` null), so its line is a visit path's and copies only the visit's own promised price, if the way has one (B4-101: 'Only the visit's own price is promised, if it has one, from Sam's settings'); never on the photos way, whose line K-30 holds to a quote option (booking_lines: a photos path if and only if quote_option_id), and a see-first quote has none priced. A service with no visit-path way is open gap 86. The row's question, 'Reply 1 Book a visit, 2 No thanks', is a K-04 open question (step A4-94 or B4-101, those two options as sent, its versions_read the quote and the link) with its question clock row, both written in the commit that writes the row (in the owner's turn; from the portal, by default 72's clock turn), waiting until the row's first delivery receipt arms the clock (C-055): the step prints no Clock line, so step 9's default holds (arch 02 line 127: with no visit yet, 8pm the day after delivery), and at the deadline the step's safe default, nothing booked. '1 Book a visit' goes on to that visit's times; '2 No thanks' books nothing.
80. **New, the owner's changed price (round 2, ruling 1):** '2 Change it' carries the new price or options (decide's money field in the owner's turn, re-parsed from their own words under limit 6; the page's field on the portal), and code stores it, with its version, as the pending change before the read-back: in a turn on the read-back's K-04 open question, written in the same commit at version 1 (its read-back words carry the pending price; its versions_read the quote, its options and the owner's ask); on the portal with the page server's session (K-21), as v1.1 default 52 keeps the Today page's read-back. K-03's {{change.price}} ('the pending choice's price field') reads that stored pending price. The read-back's exact yes ('1 Send it') applies it to K-30 quote_options in place (versions raised, the quote's too), answers the owner's ask change with the read-back's question (K-30 owner_oks.open_question_id), and goes on to setPrice photos in the same commit; '2 Type another price' stores a new pending change; a stale read-back is limit 4's. Nothing goes to the customer until that yes (A4-10). Round 3 (action-layer note a): K-04 v1 open_questions holds no structured value (its options are K-03 options {id, label}, 'the labels word for word what the person saw', here only the read-back's two answers; readback is text), so in a turn the pending change is kept as the read-back's words alone, in one form code writes and reads back: B4-11's line as code builds it from the pending options (v1.1 default 52's rule), 'Send <customer> <new> for <service>, not <old>? / Reply 1 Send it, 2 Type another price.', where <new> and <old> list the options in order, each as '<label> <price>' (the label left out when null), each price in K-03 v1's money words ('$600'; a range '$80 to $100'), joined by '; '. At the yes, code reads the options back from <new> and builds the words again from them: words that do not build back the same, or a quote or ask that moved from the versions read, fail as a stale read-back (limit 4), and nothing is sent on a guess. A structured home for the pending change is open gap 87. Round 4 (DL-175 ruling 3; AI note a of round 3): in a turn C-118 composes the row the owner reads (arch 07 line 397) and may change a master line's words (line 401), but the owner's yes binds to the read-back's exact stored words (K-04 open_questions.readback; arch 01 line 89; limit 3), so C-118's plan for A4-10's and B4-11's read-back keeps this fixed wording word for word, with each value its yes sets as a slot filled from the stored pending change ({{change.price}}; arch 07 line 409), and the words sent are the words stored. Several options or a range in a changed price are open gap 89.
81. **New, the waitlist's 'any time' (round 2, ruling 8):** K-30 waitlist_entries.wanted is never open-ended, and no source gives a range for 'any', so 'any time' is from now to the end of the seventh day after it, in the visit's zone, else the business's (A4-19: 'join the waitlist for this week'); the caller sends that range, and when it passes the person hears once and is off the list (A4-60).
82. **New, a stopped mobile's waitlist yes (round 2, ruling 8; security GAP 2):** a stopped mobile gives no yes (RULES 7.5), so `joinWaitlist` answers textsOff with consentId null and K-01's stopLaterReply (the page shows it; in a turn a row). The ruling keeps the entry, but K-30 v1 waitlist_entries.consent_id is not null ('nothing about the list is sent without it'), so no entry can be written without that yes; meanwhile nothing is written and the request stays open, and the entry waits for a K-30 minor version (open gap 71). For the lead.

It gives no key, and A4-43's forms and A4-98's approval do not ride it.

85. **New, the meets-pro adult's yes (round 2, ruling 3):** on their own link, their mobile becomes their own K-04 handle (a phones row and contacts/add, Q-03; default 68), their yes is a K-29 texts consent for that mobile on channel page with the booking and the words shown, bound to their link by its person and booking (open gap 73), and meets_pro_person_id is set to them. A stopped mobile gets no yes without START or a fresh yes on a new booking sent from that number (RULES 7.5): no consent and the texts-off line only on their page, while their mobile and meets_pro_person_id are still written, since the yes the step waits for is theirs on their own link, and texts wait (B4-27: 'No yes from the parent: no texts').

### Defaults used (T-051, round 4)

86. **New, the option choices of the see-first question and of a changed price's read-back (round 4, DL-175 ruling 1; AI FAIL 1 of round 3):** A4-94 and B4-101, where A4-09's and B4-10's '3 See it first' ends, and A4-10 and B4-11, inside A4-09's and B4-10's '2 Change it', have no split (A-J4.md and B-J4.md list no branches for them; MASTER-FLOW-A lines 2432 to 2440 and 2470 to 2480; MASTER-FLOW-B lines 2244 to 2266), yet each asks a question with two answers ('Reply 1 Book a visit, 2 No thanks'; 'Reply 1 Send it, 2 Type another price'), written as a K-04 open question with those options as sent (defaults 79 and 80). A K-02 v1 option choice is one branch of an open question (arch 01 line 67; arch 03 lines 159 to 161), carrying the bundle's branch label word for word (K-02 v1 `choice.branch`; SPEC-master 5d), and decide answers a question only by choosing a choice the context offers (K-02 v1's named rule `optionOffered`; arch 06 line 333), so with no branch it has nothing to pick. Default, in a turn, until the K-01 minor version of open gap 88: for each such open question sent to the handle, the frontier builder (C-075) offers one K-02 option choice per option the question holds (K-04 open_questions.options, in order): its `choiceId` the option's id (K-04: the ids K-02's choices carry), `question` the open question's id, `step` the question's step, `label` and `branch` the option's label as sent, word for word, standing in for the branch label the bundle lacks, `definition` and `fields` as the table below gives them, and `stakes` high with the one reason `unknown`, since the bundle holds no branch for the builder to walk (K-02 README 'Stakes': the safer route). Decide (C-115) answers these questions only by choosing one of them, naming the question's id; a bare '1' or '2' counts only when it clearly answers the one open question (arch 03 line 187; K-02's `decide-bare-2.json`). The walk each takes is code's (arch 03 line 191: it stops at the first step that asks someone, waits or ends the journey), as the table gives it, and only '2 Type another price' carries a field. The context carries no step card for these questions, since a K-01 v1 card is a split's (its question is 'the split's q'), so decide reads them by their labels and definitions (arch 03 line 181). On the portal's Needs you and on the see-first link's page no choice is built: the page makes its own calls through K-21 (`changeReadBack` with its answer; the visit's times and save), T-061.

### Open gaps (v1.2)

54. **A home for values given before their row** (default 57): K-30 v1 has no column for a request's chosen service, way and price before the save, and K-28 v1 none for a new customer's way and age band before the name; nor does any v1.2 call write a customer row for a person served who is not the booker, so their band (round 2: `setWhoFor.response.servedAgeBand`) stays with the request. A K-30 minor version (a request's chosen service and way, or the line written earlier with its price copied at the save) and a K-28 one (v1.1 open gap 41's customer row not yet confirmed) would hold them in the database. For QUESTIONS.md; the architect's next K-30 and K-28 tasks.
55. **K-20 is not frozen:** how the Agent API keeps the token's session (the pending values, the card), binds a token (Q-09's tie) and names the agent token a saved time is held for, from the agent's own authentication (default 61, round 3), are K-20's. The door is decided at the save by default 61; recording it on the hold is open gap 85.
56. **K-21's journey 4 version (T-061):** how the booking page, the secure page and the pricing page keep the card and the pending values with their session, and how the secure page names the link's person to the server.
57. **The customer row 'once booked'** (A4-23: 'A new customer is added to the business's customer list once booked'): v1.2 writes the row in A4-23's commit, so the key's home holds it when given (arch 03 line 161); whether a request that ends unbooked keeps the row (RULES 4.10) is open. For QUESTIONS.md.
58. **An organization that books** (A4-89, B4-107): no organization entity (K-28 and K-30 open gaps); `setWhoFor` writes nothing for it.
59. **A quote for a customer who came through an AI** (A4-12, B4-13): with no open chat and no email, the price waits for their own AI or page to read it, which needs a K-20 or K-21 read; round 2's clock row (default 72) has no handle to go on for such a customer, so nothing is armed.
60. **Plans and insurance** (A4-28's plan, A4-37, A4-53, B4-62, B4-63): K-32's and K-09 v1.3's (journey 5).
61. **Sent now** (B4-91): the on-call pro and the arrival estimate come with the booking write (B6, K-09 v1.4), so `saveTime` reserves nothing for it.
62. **Repeat visits, a group, several services** (A4-30, A4-32, A4-34; B4-44, B4-46, B4-48): v1.2 saves one service on one line; a second line, a series' visits and a group's people are the booking write's (v1.4) or a later minor version's.
63. **Rule-outs, price and length questions** (A4-84, A4-50; B4-97, B4-05): their answers (a count for a per-unit or per-hour price, a length) reach the line's units only as one (`booking_lines.units` default 1) until a minor version adds the count to `saveTime`.
64. **Notices** (A4-41, B4-28): the words come from the state form (no source); the booking write (A6-10, v1.4) keeps them in K-30 booking_terms.notices_shown.
65. **Asking before showing any photos** (B4-69): no K-29 kind for that yes is known to this contract.
66. **K-30's request_ended_why** has no reason for A4-80's ending branches (default 60) or for a service not offered.
67. **K-08 kinds for journey 4's writes** (CQ-56): a service matched, who it's for, a reach card, a customer added, a price shown, a quote priced or sent, a card shown, a time saved, a waitlist entry, a link issued, an address checked, a page answer; CQ-56's default applies, before C-099 commits them.
68. **The K-05 reads journey 4's builders need** (as v1.1 open gap 44): services by the words typed, a service's ways, the price resolver for a time and person, the open-times search (K-27 hours, time off, busy blocks through shares, reservations across businesses), the OK rules in force, a person's handles, a quote and its OK, the open bookings per phone or address, a link by its code's hash, and (round 2: action-layer note c) the answered open questions default 57 keeps values on, a read-back's pending change (default 80), who a reservation is held for (K-04 reservations.for_phone_number, for_agent_token or booking_id: default 61, round 3), the pricing clock row by its id with its state and version (default 78, round 3) and a mobile's stopped flag with its newest texts row. A K-05 minor version adds them before the action layer's journey 4 tasks; until then those tasks wait (NEED), with no default read.
69. **CQ-44, restated for journey 4:** every call made alone (the AI's, the booking page's, the secure page's, the pricing page's) waits for K-05's commit with no turn (open gap 3).

From the first verification (round 2, DL-162), each with its default where one is in use. For QUESTIONS.md:

70. **K-03 link slots journey 4 lacks** (ruling 2): K-03 v1's link slots are link.secure (this reader's secure page link, which `sendSecureLink` fills, the meets-pro adult's included), link.pay, link.chat and link.chatqr; none fits the photos link (A4-07 'Add photos: rosfield.com/q/a7'), the quote link and see first's times link (A4-12 'Pick a time', A4-94 'See times') or a teen's parent link (A4-96, the business's booking page), nor the pricing page's link to the pro (A4-08 'Price it: rosfield.com/t/a7'). Price it: rosfield.com/t/a7'): no source names which pro is asked once the photos are in, and the pricing page's link has no K-03 slot (open gap 70). 78. **A filtered times card again** (action-layer note b): default 69 rebuilds the card from versions_read, but a card filtered to one person or another day (A4-16, A4-17) does not store the filter; meanwhile the action layer matches the option ids against a fresh search with the same filter, which the card's open question's options name.

80. **A range's top price** (AI note d): a quote's range (`optionPrice.upTo`, A4-08 '$80 to $100') has no K-03 slot but price; a K-03 minor version adds one.
81. **Safety reads of page and AI words** (AI note e): arch 02 line 129 reads free text from pages and AIs first; `saveSecurePage` answers, `addCustomer.name`, A4-80's `name` and `checkPlace` text carry no safety read id (only `matchService` rides `openRequest`'s). For the security reviewer and the action layer: C-113 stays v1's `recordSafetyOutcome`, source page or agent.
82. **K-21's journey 4 version** (frontend notes): the page fixtures for the booking page's calls, the person's name for `timeOffered.resourceId`, the free-cancel line and 'No free cancel', the price kind and unit (open gap 63), a stay's end and a party's size (open gap 62), A4-82's line check, and the read-back's words on Needs you (default 80) are K-21's (T-061).
83. **Checks for T-118 and K-20, K-21** (security notes a, d, e): handles, effects and written never pass to the AI or the browser; an unbound token's calls (`setWhoFor`, `checkPlace` area) stay CQ-13's; `quoteFromPhotos` price and `setPrice` photos from a page need a person; a repeated `sendSecureLink` answers no url; `overBookingLimit.reviewItemId` never leaves K-20 or K-21.
84. **The door and the approval** (default 61): if the K-26 OK rules change between the save and A4-98's link, the saved time's door stays as saved; that the approval then differs from the door is not covered by the sources. Meanwhile the hold's minutes stand and A4-98 decides from the rules then in force. Round 3: `extendTime` no longer reads the approval, so an approval that differs from the door never extends a hold (default 61; open gap 85).

From the second verification (round 3, DL-171), each with its default where one is in use. For QUESTIONS.md:

85. **The saved time's door is recorded nowhere** (ruling 1; action-layer GAP 1, runtime note a): K-05 v1 extend takes the door from its caller, since K-04 v1 keeps none on the row; K-30 v1 booking_details.door is the booking's door (ai, page, chat, business, walk_in), not the hold's, and booking_details.approval is decided at A4-98, after the save. So no frozen field tells C3's 15 minutes from an AI's standard save once the time is held for the AI's agent token. Proposed: a K-05 minor version whose reserve writes the door on the hold and whose extend reads it there (with the K-04 column it needs), or a K-30 minor version recording the hold's door on booking_details. Default meanwhile (fails closed): `extendTime` refuses every time held for an agent token (`notExtendable`), `saveTime` answers an agent caller's save extendable false on either door, and an agent-token hold's lapse texts nobody, so an AI's request the owner OKs gets neither A4-21's more time nor S12-04's text until the door is recorded and a firing with no turn can write a row (CQ-44; K-05 open gap 3). For the lead; the architect's next K-05 or K-30 task, before C-100's and C-134's journey 4 tasks build `extendTime`.
86. **An estimate visit for a service with no visit-path way** (action-layer note d; default 79): K-30's own seed (`seed-quote-owner-ok.json`, the water heater) has only the way photos|deposit|bill, though its plug-in words say 'or after a free estimate visit', so its see-first visit has no K-25 way to book on, and a photos-path line needs a quote option (K-30 booking_lines). Proposed: a K-25 way of path visit for such a service (S1-13's ways), or a K-30 minor version. Default meanwhile: for such a service nothing is booked from the see-first link or a '1 Book a visit' (B4-101's safe default, 'nothing is booked'), and at the question's deadline the owner is told (arch 02 line 127: 'At the deadline: the step's safe default, then the owner is told').
87. **A structured home for the pending change** (action-layer note a; default 80): K-04 v1 open_questions keeps the options as sent and the read-back's words, and no structured value, so in a turn the pending change lives only in the read-back's words, written and read back by default 80's form. Proposed: a K-04 minor version with a structured pending value on the open question (as v1.1 open gap 41 proposes for a BOOK's values), or a K-30 one; the architect's next K-04 or K-30 task.

From the third verification (round 4, DL-175), each with its default where one is in use. For QUESTIONS.md:

88. **The splits A4-94, B4-101, A4-10 and B4-11 lack** (ruling 2; AI FAIL 1 of round 3; default 86): master-A.json and master-B.json give these four steps no split, so K-01 v1's bundle carries no branch, Means line or card for their questions, and default 86 builds their option choices in code. Proposed: a K-01 minor version that gives the four steps their splits (each option a branch with its label word for word, its definition and the walk default 86 names, and '2 Type another price''s answer field, the new price, under a name K-01's answerField pattern admits, as default 86's `new_options` is), so the bundle carries the branches, C-075 builds these choices from it as from any other split, and default 86 is retired. The master prints no Means line for these options, and B4-101's 'booked like any visit' names no step to go to, so the definitions and '1 Book a visit''s walk reach the bundle through the compiler (C-058) or an overlay Jay approves (C-071), the playbook engineer's, in that task. The same version can list default 76's and v1.1 open gap 49's answer fields, several of whose names (`bookerAgeBand`, `parentOrGuardian`, `matchService.request.words.asTyped`, `openRequest.booker.name`) have the capitals K-01 v1's answerField name refuses. Also open until then: no v1.2 call ends the request at '2 No thanks' or at the see-first question's deadline (K-30 request_ended_why dropped, 'the customer said no or went quiet', would fit), so nothing ends it; and `J4_WALK_PASSES` names no walk from A4-94 or B4-101 to the visit's times card (A4-14, B4-15), so `givesJourneyFourKeys` refuses a turn's part stated at A4-94 or B4-101 that gives times until that walk is added to the rules file (the architect's; its name carries no version, so QA's T-118 pins it). For the lead; the architect's next K-01 task.
89. **Several options or a range in a changed price** (ruling 3; AI note b of round 3, its note d of round 2): decide's field for the new price (default 86's `new_options`) and K-03 v1's {{change.price}} each hold one money value (K-02 v1 fieldValue; K-03 `slots.json` change.price, valueType money), so a '2 Change it' or '2 Type another price' of several options or a range has no field per option, and in a turn its prices would reach K-03 as one {{change.price}} string (default 80's options joined by '; '), which no money slot holds. Proposed: answer fields per option in the K-01 minor version of open gap 88, and a K-03 minor version with a price slot per option and a range's top (with open gap 80's). Default meanwhile: a turn's change is one set price, one pending option with label and upTo null (A4-10's '85', B4-11's '600'; `j4-quote-change-turn.json`), which {{change.price}} holds; several options or a range are changed on the portal's Needs you, whose page sends `newOptions` itself and shows the read-back's words, which need no K-03 slot (K-21, open gap 82). The architect's next K-01 and K-03 tasks.

## v1.3 (T-052): journey 5

**Why** (contract-change skill item 2): 02-contracts K-09's version table, row v1.3 ("packs, plans, discounts, codes and credits; documents to sign; what is taken now (exactly one of nothing, card saved, deposit, full price; never a hold); the page done in time, or ask the owner (30 minutes; 5 for work sent now); let go"), frozen before journey 5's first task (section 9, F16), with K-14 v1 and K-32 v1 frozen first; and v1.2's pointers to it (open gap 60, plans; open gap 72, a longer saved time). **Kind:** additive, a minor version. `tool-api.v1.3.schema.json` holds every v1.2 definition under its own name: the unchanged ones by `$ref` to `tool-api.v1.2.schema.json`, which stays as it is and refers to v1.1 and v1; the changed ones (`stepKey`, `given`, `partResult`, `gateAndCommit.committed`, `gateAndCommit.response`, `gateAndCommit.exchange`, `callName`) take only new enum values. So every v1, v1.1 and v1.2 exchange is a v1.3 exchange (`npm run contracts:check` checks every v1, v1.1 and v1.2 fixture against v1.3). **Scope:** A5-01 to A5-31 and B5-01 to B5-43 (the 16 steps of each flow: A-J5.md, B-J5.md), with S12-16 to S12-18 and S12-26. K-14 v1 (the Stripe port), K-29 v1, K-30 v1, K-32 v1 (money) and K-35 v1 (documents), frozen on main, are named by table, column, call and event, never redefined.

The lead's notes for T-052 bind it: v1.3 only adds to v1.2; a K-02 choice field is a K-01 answerField, so default 93's option carries no field and no name with a capital; each default added here has its row (table "Defaults 87 to 102, one row each"), and default 93's option choice its label as sent, source and walk; Master Corrections C9, C10 and section 8 win over older master lines, so no call charges on silence and every money word a call lets Rosie say names its source (`moneyWord`; default 90). Q-22 and Q-55 are the defaults in use: Rosfield's $2 is counted at the first check-in code and collected on the monthly bill, so no journey 5 payment carries a Rosfield fee (K-14 v1 default 2; K-32 v1 default 2), and the Stripe double stands in for Stripe's test keys and staging webhook (K-14 v1 open gap 1).

### The calls v1.3 adds

Every call reads PostgreSQL's **primary, uncached**, runs under v1.1's `businessCaller` (row-level security on every K-29, K-30, K-32 and K-35 row: arch 10 line 546; K-32 v1 rule 5), names its person on the server, never from the browser's or an AI's words (K-22 v1 rule 4), takes `caller.requestId` as the key a repeat is known by, and can also answer `transient`. In a turn each write rides `gateAndCommit` as the chosen choice's effect, checked by limits 1 to 6; a call made alone commits under `caller.requestId`, waits for K-05's commit with no turn (CQ-44) and writes no outbox row. Callers are held by the schema (`dependentSchemas`).

| Call              | Gives (steps)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Callers                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Writes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `showMoneyBefore` | none: what the money step shows first (A5-01, A5-19, A5-04, A5-31; twins)                                                                                                                                                                                        | page, agent                                                                                                                                                                                                       | nothing: a read of the offers, a document, the branch (`moneyBranch`) with what is taken now, the fee and the words to tick, whose yes books it and how money is taken, Stripe down, the page's clock, and each money word's row                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `settleOffer`     | none (A5-03, A5-22, A5-21; B5-31, B5-32, B5-21)                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | credit and buy: page only; plan: page or the customer's own turn                                                                                                                                                  | credit: K-32 credits, packs.credits_used, and the line replaced by a copy paid by credit (default 94); buy: a pack with its own yes's K-29 ticked_terms consent (round 3: default 109), or a membership billed monthly with its terms' K-29 consent and first plan charge, its pending payment and K-07 stripe_charge (customer present) and the K-32 saved_cards row its later charges go on (round 2: default 105), the visit's credit (default 95); plan: '1 I agree' a K-29 plan_terms consent and K-32 memberships billed per visit, a plan billed monthly not offered (default 106), '2 Just this visit' nothing (default 96)                                                                                                                                 |
| `signDocument`    | none (A5-19, A5-20; B5-34, B5-35)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | send: the booker's page or own AI; sign: only the secure page, by the link's own person                                                                                                                           | send: K-35 documents (built), document_texts, document_signers, and each co-signer's own secure link for the booker to forward; sign: K-35 document_signatures, and with the last one documents.signed_by_all_at (default 97)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `takeMoneyBefore` | money_before (A5-05, A5-07, A5-13, A5-16; B5-03, B5-05, B5-11, B5-13; the owner's '1 Book it', A5-08, B5-06), card_saved (A5-07, A5-13; B5-05, B5-11), deposit_paid (A5-13; B5-11), paid_full (A5-16; B5-13); stages card, payLink, letGo and askOwner give none | card, askOwner: page; payLink: the customer's own AI (C3); yes: page (the tap), turn (a '1', nothing only), agent (D104's request, nothing only); letGo: page or turn; ownerAnswer: the owner's turn or Needs you | card: K-07 stripe_save_card and, for a card kept, K-32 saved_cards (the full price's kept by the stored approval on a request: round 2, default 103); payLink: K-32 payments (pending), pay_links and K-07 stripe_pay_link, the url to the AI and clock rows to the email and open chat (default 88); yes: K-30 booking_details.taken 'asked' and a K-29 texts or ticked_terms consent (defaults 89, 92), on C3's pay page standing on its live pay link in place of a ready card (round 2, default 104); letGo: the hold released, the request ended dropped (in a chat, the read-back's '2 No thanks': round 2, default 107); askOwner: K-30 owner_oks and its question's clock row (default 99); ownerAnswer: owner_oks answered, taken 'nothing' on '1 Book it' |
| `refundAtOnce`    | none (S12-26)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | a job only (C-112's reconciliation, C-101's provider-event handling)                                                                                                                                              | K-32 refunds (double_charge or second_payment, decided at once) with its K-07 stripe_refund, and two clock rows due at once that tell the customer and the owner (default 101)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

No source gives these calls a time budget: in a turn each runs inside `gateAndCommit`'s 300 ms (arch 04 line 223); alone none (open gap 11). New errors: `cardNotReady`, `documentUnsigned`, `ownYesNeeded`, `wordsDiffer`, `notOffered`, `creditsShort` and `holdEnded`. Shared shapes added: `moneyBranch` (never a hold), `moneyWord` (each money word with the row it reads) and `payLinkIssued` (C3's pay link, K-03 slot link.pay).

**No charge in journey 5 but a pack's.** On the standard door a card is saved on the page (A5-07, A5-13, A5-16's card stage) and the deposit or the full price is charged as the booking is written (A6-10) or at the owner's OK (A6-03), both K-09 v1.4's (default 87); on a request the full price's card is kept for that charge, as the stored approval decides (round 2, default 103). On C3's door the pay link's payment is written pending, the yes on the pay page comes before Stripe's form confirms it, and only K-14's paymentSucceeded makes it paid and saves a deposit's card (defaults 88, 104). A pack or a membership bought on the page is charged when bought, each only on the customer's own yes to it (a pack's K-29 ticked_terms consent, a membership's plan_terms), and a membership keeps that card for its later charges (A5-22; defaults 95, 105, 109). Nothing is ever held (`moneyBranch`; RULES 3.2), and no charge is ever made on silence (A5-08's answer has no value for it; default 99).

### Each key's one call (02-contracts section 8)

| Key          | Given by (A-J5.md, B-J5.md)                                                              | Its home (02-contracts section 8: "K-32: payments; K-30: the booking line's before"; default 89)                                                                                                                                                                                  | The one call                                                |
| ------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| money_before | A5-05, A5-07, A5-13, A5-16; B5-03, B5-05, B5-11, B5-13; and A5-08's, B5-06's '1 Book it' | K-30 booking_details.taken, set once: 'asked' at the yes, 'nothing' at the owner's '1 Book it'                                                                                                                                                                                    | `takeMoneyBefore`                                           |
| card_saved   | A5-07, A5-13; B5-05, B5-11                                                               | K-32 saved_cards, the card stage's row, saved (saved_at, set by cardSaved), which the yes names; on C3's door the row paymentSucceeded writes for a deposit's card (saved_from_payment_id), never the yes (round 2, default 104)                                                  | `takeMoneyBefore`; on C3's door none: the payment's success |
| deposit_paid | A5-13; B5-11                                                                             | The yes to the deposit under the ticked words (K-29 ticked_terms), the card saved on the standard door, the live pay link on C3's; its K-32 payment of purpose deposit is written by the booking write or the owner's OK (v1.4), or by C3's pay link, and paid only by its result | `takeMoneyBefore`                                           |
| paid_full    | A5-16; B5-13                                                                             | The yes to the full price under the ticked words, the card ready on the standard door, the live pay link on C3's; its K-32 payment of purpose full_price as deposit_paid's                                                                                                        | `takeMoneyBefore`                                           |

Each key is written in journey 5 by `takeMoneyBefore` alone, whose request description names it with its step ids, and whose response answers the keys it wrote in `gives`, typed per stage and branch (round 2: on C3's door no K-09 call gives card_saved, since its row comes from the payment's success, K-14's paymentSucceeded applied by the runtime side through K-05 v1.1, after the yes; so the pay page's deposit yes gives money_before and deposit_paid, `J5_PAY_LINK_LATER`, default 104); `showMoneyBefore`, `settleOffer`, `signDocument` and `refundAtOnce` give none, and no v1.2 call gives one. The owner's '1 Book it' (A5-08, B5-06) books it with nothing taken, A5-04's 'Nothing taken', so it gives that branch's key, money_before (default 99); card_saved, deposit_paid and paid_full are never owed after a booking that took nothing (SPEC-master line 291). The named rule `givesJourneyFiveKeys` checks every fixture that states a journey 5 step (below).

### Named rule added (`tool-api.rules.mjs`)

The seven earlier rules stay as they are; v1.3 adds one under a new name, with its tables exported beside it.

- **`givesJourneyFiveKeys`:** an exchange whose `given.stated` names a journey 5 step states exactly the keys that step gives (`J5_STEP_GIVES`, from A-J5.md and B-J5.md; for the owner's '1 Book it', the 'Nothing taken' branch's: `J5_OWNER_BOOKS`), less, for a yes on C3's pay page (`payBy` payLink), the keys the payment's success writes (`J5_PAY_LINK_LATER`: card_saved; round 2, default 104), as v1.2's rule leaves out time_saved for 'Send someone now', and its outcome wrote exactly those keys; made alone, its call is the one call that writes each key (`J5_KEY_CALL`); in a turn (`gateAndCommit`) a part gives exactly its step's keys, since no automatic step of journey 5 gives one.

The rules file's name carries no version, so the frozen-contract guard does not protect it (STATE note 25's class): QA's contract test (T-119) pins it.

### Producers and consumers of v1.3

- **Producer:** action-layer engineer: C-074 Tool API, with C-101 payments and ledger (`settleOffer`, `takeMoneyBefore`'s card and pay link, `refundAtOnce`), C-099 bookings (booking_details.taken, owner_oks, the line paid by credit), C-111 own-AI books-at-once door (the pay link, D104's yes), C-094 identity and access (the co-signers' links, the consents), C-112 reconciliation job and C-183 Stripe down, C-087 one commit per turn, C-075 frontier builder (default 93's option choice), C-088 slot filler (the money words' rows), as journey 5's tasks land.
- **Consumers:** runtime engineer: C-046 handle actor and C-054 clock rows (the owner's question, the pay link's rows, the refund's notices, the saved time's lapse at A5-06 and A5-23, the document's withdrawal), C-044 gateway (K-14's events, through K-05 v1.1, unchanged); AI engineer: C-114 context bundle, C-115 and C-116 decide (default 93; A5-08's and A5-21's splits), C-118 composer (the read-back rows, their money words from `moneyWords`, link.pay); frontend engineer, through K-21's journey 5 version (T-062): C-199 secure page (the money step, the card form, offers, signing), C-200 pay page and pay links, C-197 booking page, C-192 owner console (Needs you's answer); action-layer engineer: C-110 Agent API (`showMoneyBefore`, the pay link, D104's yes); architect: C-186 Tool API mock; QA: the contract test `tests/contract/tool-api.v1.3.test.ts` (T-119), C-140 eval harness, C-147 scenario tests.
- **Frozen before it, read here:** K-09 v1.2 (T-051), K-14 v1 (T-023), K-32 v1 (T-041), with v1.2's own (K-01 to K-05, K-07, K-08, K-16, K-17, K-22 for proof codes, K-23 to K-30, K-34, K-36, K-37) and K-35 v1 (T-044).
- **Not here:** QA's contract test `tool-api.v1.3.test.ts` (T-119); journey 5's K-21 screens (T-062); building the calls (the action layer's journey 5 tasks).

### Fixtures, v1.3 (in `fixtures/index.json` with version v1.3)

24 accepted and 17 refused (round 1's 18 and 11, round 2's 6 and 3, round 3's 0 and 3), each refused for its one fault (with that fault fixed it is accepted: checked 2026-11-09, round 2's 3 and the round 1 fixtures round 2 changed alike, and round 3's 3). Round 2 changed round 1's fixtures only where its shapes grew: every yes names `payBy` and `payLinkId` (as showMoneyBefore answered: none or cardOnPage, no pay link), every settleOffer answer names `savedCardId` (null), and `j5-full-price-yes-page.json`'s note says its card stage's keep was null, the booking made now. Round 3 changed one: `j5-buy-pack-page.json` now carries Alex's own yes to the pack (`termsYes`, the words shown) and answers its ticked_terms consent (`consentId`).

- **The money step on the page, flow A** (after v1.2's `j4-page-answers-flow-a.json`): `j5-money-before-page.json` (A5-04: the haircut's card branch, the $20 fee, Alex's own yes, each money word's row), `j5-card-form-page.json` (A5-07's card form: the save effect and the saved card row), `j5-card-saved-yes-page.json` (A5-07's yes: money_before, card_saved), `j5-deposit-yes-page.json` (A5-13: the Groom's package's $40 deposit, charged as the booking is written), `j5-full-price-yes-page.json` (A5-16: the $55 Signature cut).
- **The AI's doors:** `j5-pay-link-agent.json` (MC C3: Dee's AI gets the pay link, due at the hold's 15-minute end) and `j5-nothing-yes-agent.json` (D104: Dee's AI books the style consult on its request).
- **Nothing taken, and a no:** `j5-nothing-yes-turn.json` (A5-05's '1' in Alex's own chat, with his texts consent), `j5-nothing-yes-texts-off-page.json` (Ben T.'s stopped mobile: booked, no consent, the texts-off line), `j5-let-go-page.json` (A5-05's no: the time let go, dropped).
- **Ask the owner:** `j5-ask-owner-page.json` (A5-31's 'Ask Sam first': no card works) and `j5-owner-book-turn.json` (A5-08's '1 Book it' in Sam's turn: taken 'nothing', money_before).
- **Offers:** `j5-buy-pack-page.json` (A5-22: the yoga studio's pack of 5 visits), `j5-use-credit-page.json` (A5-03: a later class on a credit) and `j5-plan-agree-turn.json` (A5-21: Owen H.'s '1 I agree').
- **A document, flow B:** `j5-document-send-page.json` (B5-34: the $640 water heater contract) and `j5-document-sign-page.json` (B5-35: Alex signs, all signed).
- **S12-26:** `j5-refund-second-charge-job.json` (Dee W.'s second $20 charge refunded at once by reconciliation's job).
- **Refused:** `invalid/refuse-j5-deposit-yes-from-agent.json` (an AI's request as the yes to a deposit), `invalid/refuse-j5-book-on-silence.json` (the owner's deadline sent as his answer), `invalid/refuse-j5-deposit-gives-paid-full.json` (a deposit's yes giving paid_full), `invalid/refuse-j5-hold-branch.json` (a hold on the card), `invalid/refuse-j5-pay-link-from-page.json` (the pay link asked from a page), `invalid/refuse-j5-plan-yes-from-agent.json` (an AI's yes to a plan), `invalid/refuse-j5-sign-for-someone.json` (an AI signing), `invalid/refuse-j5-refund-from-console.json` (a refund at once that waits for a person), `invalid/refuse-j5-yes-texts-off-with-consent.json` (a stopped mobile's yes holding a consent), `invalid/refuse-j5-deposit-card-not-kept.json` (a deposit's card not kept) and, by the rule `givesJourneyFiveKeys`, `invalid/refuse-j5-deposit-stated-as-full-price.json` (a deposit's yes stated as A5-16).
- **Round 2 (DL-252), one or more per ruling:** (1) `j5-full-price-card-request-page.json` (A5-16's card form on a request Sam OKs: keep null, the card kept by the stored approval); (2) C3's pay page, in its order after `j5-pay-link-agent.json`: `j5-pay-page-money-before-page.json` (showMoneyBefore, payBy payLink), `j5-pay-page-yes-page.json` (the yes on the live pay link: money_before, paid_full) and `j5-pay-page-deposit-yes-page.json` (a deposit's yes there: money_before and deposit_paid, card_saved left to the payment's success); (3) `j5-buy-membership-page.json` (the yoga studio's monthly membership bought on the page, billed monthly, its saved card answered); (4) `j5-let-go-turn.json` (A5-05's '2 No thanks' in Alex's own chat: letGo from the turn).
- **Refused in round 2:** `invalid/refuse-j5-full-price-keep-from-caller.json` (A5-16's keep sent by the caller), `invalid/refuse-j5-pay-page-yes-without-pay-link.json` (a yes on payBy payLink naming no live pay link) and `invalid/refuse-j5-membership-buy-no-saved-card.json` (a membership bought on the page answered with no saved card).
- **Refused in round 3 (DL-257):** `invalid/refuse-j5-pack-buy-without-yes.json` (a pack bought on the page with no yes of Alex's own), `invalid/refuse-j5-pack-buy-no-consent.json` (a pack's buy answered with no consent) and, for the security reviewer's note a, `invalid/refuse-j5-card-yes-no-consent.json` (A5-07's yes answered with no ticked_terms consent).

Names and values come from the master's sample cast and fixture set v1's demo shops (Alex M., Sam, Riley, Dee W., Ben T., Owen H.; Lee's Barbershop's $35 haircut with its $20 fee, the $140 Groom's package with its $40 deposit, the $55 Signature cut and hot towel shave, the style consult; the yoga studio's pack of 5 visits for $160 and its $35 Hot Flow class; Owen's haircut plan, $35 every 4 weeks; Lee Home Services' $640 water heater contract; Dee's $20 charged twice; October and November 2026), never a real person (rule 9). Ids reuse K-14's, K-32's and v1.2's where the rows are theirs (`eff-alex-haircut-save-card`, `card-alex-grooms-package`, `pack-alex-yoga-5-visits`, `pay-alex-hot-flow-pack`, `eff-alex-hot-flow-pack`, `credit-alex-hot-flow-1`, `plan-owen-haircut`, `consent-owen-plan-terms`, `refund-dee-second-charge`, `ledger-dee-second-charge`, `eff-reconcile-dee-second-charge-refund`, `booking-agent-alex-oct21`, `line-agent-alex-1`, `rsv-agent-alex-riley-sat`). These are examples, not from the sources (default 48's rule): every other booking, line, reservation, consent, document, signer, signature, owner OK, clock row, pay link and payment id, the words shown and ticked, the pay link's placeholder code, and the times of day the steps do not print; round 2's too: the yoga studio's monthly membership ($120 a month, 8 classes) and its terms' words, the plan charge, saved card and membership ids, Dee's Groom's package and Signature cut visit times, and the pay page's words; and round 3's: the pack's words shown and its consent id.

### Defaults used (T-052)

87. **New, where the money is taken (S13-55, the home step, over A5-13's and A5-16's own words; MC section 2 item 2):** S13-55 says "What's due now is taken once the booking is written, so a time lost in that moment costs nothing" and "On a request, nothing is charged for this visit while the OK is pending. The card is saved"; K-14 v1's `charge` takes "a deposit (A5-13) or the full price (A5-16) when the booking is written (A6-10)" (its `deposit.json`: "The booking is written, then the deposit is charged on Alex's saved card while he is still on the page"), and K-32 v1's `seed-payment-deposit` has the card saved on the page, then the deposit charged as the booking is written. So on the standard door journey 5 writes no charge: the card stage readies the card, the yes records the customer's yes to the amount, and the booking write (A6-10) or the owner's OK (A6-03), both K-09 v1.4, writes the payment pending with its stripe_charge effect, customer present for a booking made now. Only a pack or a membership bought on the page is charged in journey 5 (A5-22: "charged when bought"; default 95), and C3's door takes its money by the pay link (default 88).
88. **MC C3, the pay link (D104):** on the customer's own AI's booking that books at once with a deposit or the full price due now, the AI gets the pay link for the customer, and so do their email and an open chat: `takeMoneyBefore` payLink, agent only, decided from stored facts, never the AI's word (the hold held for its agent token, K-04 reservations.for_agent_token; booking_details.approval now; the lines' before deposit or full: v1.2 open gaps 84 and 85 still hold for the hold's own door). It writes the K-32 payment pending with its K-07 stripe_pay_link effect (K-14 payLink; saveCardForLater for a deposit, since "the card stays saved"), the pay_links row (given_via ai, and email where there is one; due_at the hold's own end, 15 minutes, never extended; only the code's sha256: K-32 default 21), the url to the AI once, never logged, and clock rows due at once on the customer's email handle and open chat's handle, whose clock turns fill link.pay by K-32 default 21's code (v1.2 default 72's way). Paid within the 15 minutes, the booking write books it (v1.4); not paid, closePayLink cancels it at the deadline, the time goes back, nothing is booked or charged, and nobody is chased (MC C3). A card to save on C3's door goes on the secure page the AI already has (v1.2 `sendSecureLink` via ai), through the card stage; the pay page and the secure page take the customer's own yes (default 91). On the pay page the caller's person is the pay link's (K-32 pay_links.person_id), set by the page server from the link it found by its code's hash, never by the browser, and the yes comes before Stripe's form confirms the payment ("Never: take money before the price and the yes to it"). Round 2 states the pay page's whole order, the yes's precondition and where card_saved lives on this door: default 104.
89. **New, what the four keys hold (02-contracts section 8; SPEC-master section 5: "Keys the money path gives (the checker seeds and checks them between parts)"):** money_before is K-30 booking_details.taken, set once by the yes ('asked', as the service asks) or by the owner's '1 Book it' ('nothing'); card_saved is the K-32 saved_cards row the card stage wrote for the booking, saved by cardSaved, which the yes needs first on the standard door (`cardNotReady`), and on C3's door the row paymentSucceeded writes for a deposit's card after the yes (saved_from_payment_id), which the yes never gives (round 2, default 104); deposit_paid and paid_full are the customer's own yes to that amount under the words they ticked (K-29 ticked_terms), with the card ready on the standard door or the live pay link on C3's. The K-32 payment of purpose deposit or full_price, which K-32 v1's README reads as these keys' home ("a paid payment"), is written by the booking write or the owner's OK (v1.4), or on C3's door by the pay link, and becomes paid only by its effect's result or paymentSucceeded (K-32 rule 5): open gap 90.
90. **New, money words name their source (the lead's note on T-052; S13-55: "After the money step, messages speak only of what it took" and "A message never says deposit paid, paid in full, charged or refunded unless the record says it happened"; RULES 3.14; K-03 v1 slots.json):** every v1.3 answer that lets Rosie, a page or an AI say a money word lists it in `moneyWords`: the K-03 record slot (pay.asks, pay.price, pay.took, pay.left, pay.cancel, pay.late, pay.noshow, pay.back, pay.failed, clock.pay) with the table, column and row the slot filler (C-088) reads, and limit 7's claim check (C-091) holds the words to them. A word that says money was taken or given back reads only a K-32 payments row's state, when paid, or a refunds row's state, when succeeded, never a gives key: after A5-13's yes the deposit reads as due as the booking is written (booking_lines.deposit_cents and booking_details.taken), never as paid.
91. **New, whose yes and how (A5-05: "Never: count a reaction, an ok to something else, or someone else's AI as their yes"; A3-03; S13-55; MC C3):** the customer's own yes books it: on the page, with the words the page server showed from `showMoneyBefore`, which code builds again from the rows read and compares (`wordsDiffer` otherwise; v1.1 default 52's rule), or, for nothing taken, a '1' in their own chat answering the read-back's open question (default 93). A card, a deposit and the full price take the page's yes only ("cust → pay page"). An AI's call is never the customer's yes (`ownYesNeeded`), except on D104's door: nothing to pay or save, booked at once on the AI's request (booking_details.approval now, the hold held for the AI's agent token), where its request is the yes. A request the owner OKs (approval ownerok) keeps the master's own rules: the customer's own yes.
92. **New, the yes's consents (A5-05: "The yes records their OK to messages about this booking"; A5-07: "The exact words the customer ticked are stored with the booking"; RULES 7.5; MC C1):** nothing taken: a K-29 texts consent for the booking, bound to the yes's words and their sha256; a stopped mobile gets none, only K-01's stopLaterReply (on the page shown by the page), since a page's tap is neither START nor a fresh yes sent from that number (v1.2 default 82's rule). A card, a deposit and the full price: a K-29 ticked_terms consent with the exact words ticked and their sha256, which the booking write's terms snapshot names (K-30 booking_terms.terms_consent_id, v1.4). D104's AI request records no consent. Whether the pay page's yes also records the OK to messages is open gap 95. Round 3: a yes to a card, a deposit or the full price always answers its ticked_terms consent (`takeMoneyBefore.response`; the security reviewer's note a), and a pack bought on the page writes its own yes as a ticked_terms consent too (default 109).
93. **New, A5-05's and B5-03's '1 Book it' in a chat (A5-05: "One yes books it: the page's Book it, or 1 in reply to the link"; T-051 round 4's lesson):** A5-05 and B5-03 have no split (A-J5.md, B-J5.md list no branches), yet ask a question with one answer. In a text chat the read-back row ('Book this …? … Reply 1 Book it.') is written by the commit that reaches A5-05: in a turn its row, with a K-04 open question whose options are '1 Book it' and, since round 2, '2 No thanks', as sent (default 107), and whose versions_read are the rows A5-04 read; from a page's commit (the secure page's last answer), by v1.2 default 72's clock row due at once on the customer's handle, only into an open chat (MC C9). Until the K-01 minor version of open gap 94, the frontier builder (C-075) offers each option as a K-02 option choice, as v1.2 default 86 does, and decide (C-115) picks only one of them; a bare '1' or '2' counts only when it clearly answers that one open question (arch 03 line 187). Their `stakes` are high with the one reason `unknown`, as default 86's, since the bundle holds no branch to walk. No answer of journey 5 in a turn carries a field: each is an option choice (A5-05's '1 Book it' and '2 No thanks', A5-08's and A5-21's branches), so no K-01 answerField name is needed, and none has a capital (K-01 v1 answerField's pattern `^[a-z][a-z0-9_.]*$`; T-051 round 4). The money a yes takes is never decide's: the amounts come from the rows A5-04 read (`showMoneyBefore`). Round 2 (DL-252 ruling 4; AI FAIL 1): the read-back's open question holds a second option, '2 No thanks', for A5-05's 'no', which decide picks for a no in the customer's own chat and which walks to `letGo` from the turn (default 107), so its options are '1 Book it' and '2 No thanks', both as sent; A5-05's other answers ('Yes, make it a fade', 'Yes, but I'm 16') are no option of it (open gap 103). Its rows, each with its label as sent, source and walk:

| ------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- |
| A5-05 (B5-03): nothing taken, the yes, in a chat | '1 Book it'                                                                                               | 'a clear yes to book it, with nothing taken; a yes with a but is not a yes yet' (A5-05)                                                                            | A5-05, B5-03 (MASTER-FLOW-A lines 3034 to 3046) | `takeMoneyBefore` yes, nothing, from the turn (YesInTurn): the question closed answered, taken 'asked', the texts consent, money_before; A5-31's 'Done: the yes', then A6-01 (v1.4); a yes with a 'but' ('Yes, make it a fade', 'Yes, but I'm 16') is no option of it and books nothing (round 2, open gap 103) | none     |

94. **New, a credit's line (A5-03: "With only this service, this sets the booking as paid by a credit"; S13-55: "A pack credit covers its own service only ... It adds nothing to what's due or paid now"; K-32 v1 rule 10: "a credit's line is paid by credit"):** K-30 v1 copies booking_lines.paid_by when the time is saved (A4-21, B4-23), before A5-01 offers the credit, and never changes it. So `settleOffer` credit (and a buy's visit credit) replaces the open line, before the booking is written, by a copy paid by credit with the same service, way, price and fee (the old line removed with no change row: K-30 booking_lines.state "replaced before the booking was written"; v1.1 open gap 46's default), the credit naming the new line and the pack's credits_used raised in the same commit. The money taken now leaves the line out. Open gap 91.
95. **New, packs and memberships bought on the page (A5-22; K-32 v1 defaults 15 and 28):** what is sold, and at what terms, comes from the page server, built from the plug-in's packs fact and the owner's settings, never the browser's (open gap 92). The card is typed on the page through the card stage with keep false (no saved card row); the pack is charged at once with the customer present (K-14 charge, purpose pack, customerPresent true), and a membership is its first plan charge, made with the customer present, with its own terms' yes (K-29 plan_terms). "Try another card" on a decline (RULES 3.11); a pack whose payment declined keeps its row (K-32 v1 open gap), and a membership no card pays ends not_paid with its credit given back (K-32 default 28). The visit's credit is written in the same commit (default 94). A barbershop sells its packages only at the shop. Round 2: a membership bought on the page is billed monthly and keeps that card for its later charges, its saved_cards row written by the buy (default 105). Round 3: a pack is charged only on the customer's own yes to it, its K-29 ticked_terms consent written in the buy's commit and answered (default 109), which C-018's hourly check finds through the event log (default 110).
96. **New, the plan's yes (A5-21):** '1 I agree' writes the K-29 plan_terms consent bound to the terms' exact words and the K-32 memberships row (kind plan, billed per_visit, terms_consent_id; a plan billed monthly is not offered in v1.3: round 2, default 106); '2 Just this visit' writes nothing and the visit still books (A5-21's safe default). In the customer's own chat or on their page, never an AI's ("Never a yes relayed in a chat by someone else's AI"). A copy of the terms goes back the way they picked: in a turn the reply; on the page the page; by email through v1.2 default 72's clock row. The plan's details chosen before the times (A4-28, A4-37) are kept nowhere yet (open gap 92).
97. **New, a booking's document (A5-19, A5-20, A5-23; B5-34 to B5-36; K-35 v1 "Built documents and signatures"):** `signDocument` send builds it from the price and the parts the plug-in and the owner require, its words per language from a checked text, its signers the booker and each co-signer the booker names (a K-04 person first), each co-signer's own K-30 secure link (purpose secure_page) given to the booker's page or AI to forward, never texted or emailed (MC C9; v1.2 default 83's way). sign takes one signer on the secure page from their own link, a tap or a typed name (sealed), with the hash of the words read; the last signature sets documents.signed_by_all_at, and the money step waits for it (`documentUnsigned`). Not signed by the saved time's end, the clock firing (C-134) releases the time, withdraws the document not_signed_in_time and ends the request not_signed (K-30), and the customer, and in flow B the owner, are told (B5-36). A5-23's and B5-36's "one reminder before the saved time ends" has no clock row of its own in v1.3 (the hold's row fires at its end), and how long the time stays saved while they sign is not K-05 v1's to give: both are open gap 93.
98. **New, the longer holds K-05 v1 cannot give yet (A5-08: "The time stays saved until Sam's deadline: 30 minutes"; A5-20: "minutes, or up to a day"; S12-17: "up to 2 hours, and its 10 minutes stop until Stripe is back"):** K-05 v1 has the doors (ownerOk up to 30 minutes, document up to 1,440, planCheck up to 60) but no call that moves a held time onto them or pauses it (only extend's one 10 minutes; v1.2 open gap 85 for the door), and MC C3 gives C3's door none. Meanwhile the hold keeps K-05 v1's end, and an owner's answer, a last signature or Stripe's return after it ended is checked against the times again before anything books ("A late yes is checked first: is the time still free?"; limit 5; `holdEnded`), so nothing is booked or taken on a lost time; while the owner's nothing-taken question waits, the saved time's clock firing releases the time but leaves the request open for the owner's deadline. Open gap 93.
99. **New, the owner's ask and answer (A5-08, B5-06; K-30 v1 owner_oks about nothing_taken):** `askOwner` from the page when no card works for a card-saved service or Stripe is down (S12-17: "Sam is asked at once"), never on C3's door (MC C3: Sam's OK "do[es] not apply to this door"), writes the owner_oks row, answer_by 30 minutes of the owner's text hours, or 5 minutes at any hour for a job sent now (B5-06), and one clock row due at once on the owner's handle whose clock turn writes A5-08's question with its open question (a page's commit writes no outbox row: v1.2 default 66's way); the customer hears when they'll know on the page. `ownerAnswer` takes the owner's or a permitted admin's own answer in their turn or on Needs you: '1 Book it' answers book in K-08's ownerYes shape and sets taken 'nothing' ("booked with no money taken, so no fee can be charged"), giving money_before as A5-04's 'Nothing taken' (`J5_OWNER_BOOKS`); '2 Let it go' answers let_go, ends the request declined (K-30: the owner said no), releases the hold and tells the customer. Silence never books: the answer has no value for it, and no answer by the deadline is the question clock's firing (C-055), which writes owner_oks no_answer, ends the request no_answer, releases the time and tells the customer (A5-08's safe default; S13-55: "Rosie NEVER makes a new charge on silence").
100.  **New, Stripe down (S12-16 to S12-18; K-07 v1 retry rule 8; K-14 v1 'A try past 23 hours'):** `showMoneyBefore.stripeDown` is true while any K-07 effect of the business on port stripe is in state outage; the page says "Payments are down for a few minutes", never a decline; the card stage's effect waits in its outage and runs once when Stripe is back; the owner is asked at once (`askOwner`, why stripeDown), and a no from the owner follows A5-08. Once Stripe is back the customer's one text with the link to finish is the secure link's (v1.2 `sendSecureLink`, into an open chat only); the 2 hours are open gap 93. After the visit (S12-18) the charge is journey 9's and waits the same way.
101.  **New, refunds at once (S12-26; K-32 v1 refunds, rule 10):** `refundAtOnce`, by a job only (C-112's reconciliation for a double charge, C-101's provider-event handling for a second payment), writes the refund for the whole second charge, decided at once, via stripe, to the person who paid, with its stripe_refund effect (K-14 reason duplicate), and two clock rows due at once (a job's commit writes no outbox row): one on the customer's open chat, else their email, and one on the owner's handle, whose clock turns write S12-26's two rows. Their pay.back reads the refund's own state, so "The second charge is refunded" is said only once it succeeded (S13-55), and a row composed before then says only what the record says (K-03's fallback). A double charge's stripe_refund effect is K-05 v1.1 reconciliationRefund's, the writer K-07 v1 names, which writes only the effect and its log entries ('Its ledger line and telling Alex and Sam are not this call's': its fixture); so the refunds row and the two notices' clock rows wait for the commit with no turn (open gap 99; CQ-44), and a second payment's refund also waits for the K-05 and K-07 minor versions K-32 v1 names (open gap 96) (round 2: the action-layer engineer's and the runtime engineer's note a).
102.  **K-32 v1 default 19 (QUESTIONS.md M5.3), discount codes stay off (A5-02, B5-33):** K-23 v1 holds no codes and K-32 v1 no use of one, so `showMoneyBefore.offers.discountCodes` is always false, no call takes a code, and a code from an AI is never taken on its word (A5-01); K-23's and K-32's minor versions add them.

The card is used only after the OK"; A6-03 (line 3183): "If a deposit or the full price is due now, it's charged as the booking is written"; K-32 v1 saved_cards ("for a booking (the fee, a rest charged at DONE, a deposit or full price at the owner's OK)"; saved_cards_booking_idx) and K-32 default 15 ("unless the booking keeps it for later"). So at A5-16 and B5-13 the card stage keeps the card when the stored approval is ownerok and keeps none when the booking is made now (approval now: the full price is charged on the card typed as the booking is written, K-14 full-price.json), decided by the call from K-30 booking_details.approval, which A4-98 set and which is never decided again (A6-01), never by the caller: the request's `keep` is null at those steps, and the answer's `card.kept` says which. A kept card's K-32 saved_cards row names the booking with its save effect and is saved by cardSaved; the yes still gives A5-16's own keys, money_before and paid_full, never card_saved (A-J5.md); A6-03 (K-09 v1.4) charges the full price on it at the owner's OK. A request the owner declines (A6-02's '3 Decline') or one never made removes the card at once (S13-55, line 7351: "A booking that's declined or never made removes it at once"; K-14 removeCard; K-32 saved_cards.removed_why not_booked), written by the commit that ends it (K-09 v1.4's decline; the saved time's clock firing for a hold that lapses).

Paid or card saved within 15 minutes: booked"; A5-04 (MASTER-FLOW-A line 3028): "Never: take money before the price and the yes to it"; K-14 v1 "Pay links" (pageIntent; paymentSucceeded with paymentMethodId; saveCardForLater) and K-32 v1 "K-14's results and events, row by row" (payLink: "for saveCardForLater a saved_cards row from paymentMethodId ... (saved_from_payment_id)"). On C3's door the calls run in this order: (1) `takeMoneyBefore` payLink from the customer's own AI (default 88); (2) on the pay page, `showMoneyBefore` (payBy payLink), its person the pay link's (K-32 pay_links.person_id), set by the page server from the link it found by its code's sha256 (K-32 default 21), never by the browser; (3) the yes there, `takeMoneyBefore` yes with `payBy` payLink, naming the booking's live pay link (`payLinkId`: ended_at null, its payment pending) in place of a ready card, so `cardNotReady` never applies on this door; a link that is not live is refused (holdEnded once it closed at its deadline; notFound when it is not this booking's), and the answer names that link with no url and no clock row, since nothing is given or armed again; (4) Stripe's form on the page confirms the payment with the client secret the page server reads by pageIntent (K-14); (5) K-14's paymentSucceeded makes the payment paid (K-32 rule 5: only the runtime side does), ends the link paid, and for a deposit's card kept (saveCardForLater: "the card stays saved", A5-13) writes its K-32 saved_cards row (saved_from_payment_id); (6) the booking write (A6-10, K-09 v1.4). So on this door card_saved comes from the payment's success, never from the yes: a deposit's yes gives money_before and deposit_paid, the full price's money_before and paid_full (`J5_PAY_LINK_LATER`; `givesJourneyFiveKeys`), and every key A5-13 gives holds before the booking write. Not paid by the pay link's due_at, the hold's own end: the hold's saved-time clock row fires (K-05 item 8; the minute sweep's for an agent token), its firing closes the payment by closePayLink (K-14 "Pay links"; K-32 pay_links.due_at), the time goes back, nothing is booked or charged, no card is saved, and nobody is chased (MC C3). The 15 minutes are never extended.
