### Round 3 (DL-257): what changed

The second verification (round 2, 144a476) had the PASS of QA and the four consumers and one FAIL, the security reviewer's: a pack bought on the page was charged with no bound yes (PackItem carried no yes, and its answer named no consent), so C-018's hourly check would find no yes behind the pack's payment, which names no booking, and K-32 v1 rule 10 would either refund every pack at once as an unbound charge or let a card charge escape the bound-yes rule. The lead's ruling (`ops/cards/T-052.md`, "Round 3 rulings") closes exactly that, in v1.3 itself, still only adding to v1.2 (every v1, v1.1 and v1.2 fixture still passes v1.3), and every round 2 PASS point stands.

| Ruling                                         | Where in the schema and fixtures                                                                                                                                                                         | Defaults, gaps    |
| ---------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- |
| A pack carries the customer's own yes          | PackItem `termsYes` (`readBackYes`, MembershipItem's form; `wordsDiffer` applies), required; `settleOffer.request`'s description; `j5-buy-pack-page.json`; `invalid/refuse-j5-pack-buy-without-yes.json` | 109               |
| The buy writes its K-29 consent and answers it | `settleOffer.response` (every buy answers `consentId`: a pack's ticked_terms, a membership's plan_terms); `j5-buy-pack-page.json`; `invalid/refuse-j5-pack-buy-no-consent.json`                          | 109               |
| How C-018 finds a pack payment's bound yes     | This README                                                                                                                                                                                              | 110; open gap 105 |
| Cheap note a: a yes to money names its consent | `takeMoneyBefore.response` (a yes to a card, a deposit or the full price answers `consentId`); `invalid/refuse-j5-card-yes-no-consent.json`                                                              | 92                |
| Cheap note b: consents in `written`            | This README (below): not listed, as K-05 v1 and K-29 v1 are frozen                                                                                                                                       | none              |

**Cheap note b, kept as v1 and v1.1 keep it.** `written` lists K-05 v1 `rowRef`s, each a row with the version its commit set (`version`, an integer from 1), and a K-29 consent row is only appended and carries no version (K-29 v1 rule 2: "`consents` is only appended and carries none"). So no answer lists its consent in `written`, and `consentId` names it, as v1's `fee-consent.json` ("The fee_ok consent row is never edited and carries no version, so it is not listed in written") and v1.1's `j3-confirm-business-booking-yes.json` already do. Listing it would state a version K-29 never gives; what binds each money yes's answer to its consent is cheap note a's rule, which holds `consentId` to an id.

### Defaults used (T-052, round 3)

109. **New, a pack's own yes and its consent (round 3, DL-257; the security reviewer's FAIL 1):** K-32 v1 README line 43 ("K-29 conversations and consent (frozen) owns every yes"), line 95 (C-018 reads "each charge's bound yes") and rule 10 (line 147: a payment "with no bound yes behind it" is refunded at once, `unbound_charge`, "found by C-018's hourly check and refunded by C-016"); arch 08 line 447 ("an unbound charge is refunded at once (S12-26)"); S13-55 (MASTER-FLOW-A line 7337): "A new or higher amount, an extra or a plan needs the customer's own yes"; A5-04 (line 3028): "Never: take money before the price and the yes to it, or more than the terms say"; A5-07: "The exact words the customer ticked are stored with the booking"; K-29 v1 consents (kinds texts, photos_shown, ticked_terms, plan_terms, fee_ok and health_agreement; `consents_ticked_terms_has_booking`). A pack bought on the page (`settleOffer` buy, PackItem) carries the customer's own yes to it, `termsYes`, in MembershipItem's form (`readBackYes`): on the page the tap, with the words the page server showed for the pack, its service, its credits and its price charged now, which code builds again from the item and compares (`wordsDiffer` otherwise; v1.1 default 52's rule; default 91), never an AI's (`ownYesNeeded`; a buy is the page's only). The buy's commit writes that yes as a K-29 consent of kind **ticked_terms**, the kind K-29 v1 gives the words a customer ticks for money taken on a card (A5-07), since plan_terms is a plan's or a membership's own yes to repeat charges (A5-21; K-32 memberships.terms_consent_id) and a pack is charged once: the exact words and their sha256, the person, the business, channel page, and the booking the pack was bought in, which `consents_ticked_terms_has_booking` requires (the request's `bookingId`, though the pack's payment names no booking). It answers that consent as `consentId`, which `settleOffer.response` holds to an id for every buy, and the pack's charge is written only with it. It is not the booking's own terms: the booking write's terms snapshot names the money step's yes by its id (K-30 booking_terms.terms_consent_id, v1.4; default 92), never the pack's.
110. **New, how C-018 finds a pack payment's bound yes (round 3, DL-257; K-32 v1 rule 10, lines 95 and 147):** a pack's payment names no booking (K-32 payments of purpose pack, booking_id null; A5-22, MASTER-FLOW-A line 2945: "If the booking isn't confirmed, the pack stays bought and its credits stay usable"), K-32 v1 gives a pack no consent column (a membership has `terms_consent_id`, a pack none), and K-08 v1 reads the hourly checks "from the event log and traces" (`invariantRun`, `noChargeWithoutBoundYes`), whose rows name the exact yes behind each write (`approved_by`: K-08 `approvalConsentRow`, "its id in K-29"; K-04 event_log.approved_by). So the buy's commit appends, beside its `consent.given` row, the `effect.created` row of the payment's K-07 stripe_charge effect (both K-08 v1 kinds; a pack's own kinds are open gap 97), with `approved_by` {kind consentRow, consent_id} naming the pack's ticked_terms consent. C-018 goes from each charge to its yes by that row, never by payments.booking_id: the payment, its effect (payments.effect_id; one effect per payment, payments_effect_key), that effect's `effect.created` row, and the consent its `approved_by` names. It holds the charge bound when that K-29 row exists, is of the payment's business and payer (consents.person_id = payments.payer_person_id) and of kind ticked_terms, and the amount charged is the price of the pack the payment bought (packs.payment_id, unique; payments.amount_cents = packs.price_cents), the words having been compared with the item at the yes (`wordsDiffer`). A pack payment whose effect row names no such consent is an unbound charge: C-016 refunds it at once (`unbound_charge`, decided at_once), naming that payment with booking_id null, as a pack's refund does (K-32 refunds.booking_id), and both are told (S12-26). A membership's first charge is also found by its plan charge (payments.plan_charge_id, then the membership's terms_consent_id), and a booking's deposit or full price by its booking's terms snapshot (K-30 booking_terms.terms_consent_id, v1.4). Open gap 105.

### Defaults 109 and 110, one row each

| Default                                | Source                                                                                   | The walk (what it makes happen)                                                                                              |
| -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 109 A pack's own yes and its consent   | K-32 README lines 43, 95, rule 10; arch 08 line 447; S13-55; A5-04; A5-07; K-29 consents | the page's own yes to the pack with the words shown; a ticked_terms consent in the buy's commit, answered as consentId       |
| 110 How C-018 finds a pack's bound yes | K-32 rule 10, lines 95, 147; K-08 invariantRun, approved_by; K-07 effects; K-32 refunds  | the charge's effect.created row approved by the pack's consent; none behind it: an unbound charge, refunded at once by C-016 |

### Open gaps (v1.3, round 3)

From the second verification (round 2, DL-257), with its default. For QUESTIONS.md:

105. **No column binds a pack to its yes** (default 110; round 3): K-32 v1 gives a membership its yes by a column (memberships.terms_consent_id) but a pack none, and a pack's payment names no booking, so C-018 finds a pack's bound yes only through the event log's `approved_by` on its charge's `effect.created` row (default 110), and K-08 v1 has no kind of its own for a pack bought (open gap 97). Proposed: a K-32 minor version adding packs.terms_consent_id, set once by the buy's commit and held to its business as memberships.terms_consent_id is (K-32 rule 5), so C-018 reads a pack's yes by a column, as a membership's; then default 110 is retired. The architect's next K-32 task, before C-018's bound-yes check first meets a pack (journey 5's tasks).

## v1.4 (T-053): journey 6

**Why** (contract-change skill item 2): 02-contracts K-09's version table, row v1.4 ("book now or ask the owner (accept, another time, decline, no answer in 4 text hours); the on-call pro (5 minutes); write the booking after a fresh free/busy read, charging what is due; one pay link after a failed charge; the confirmation with a new check-in code, its chat link and QR going on the page and in the email (C9); tell the pro"), frozen before journey 6's first task (section 9, F16), with K-14 v1, K-16 v1, K-30 v1 and K-32 v1 frozen first; and v1.3's pointers to it (default 87: the deposit or the full price charged as the booking is written or at the owner's OK; default 104's step 6: C3's booking write; default 103: a declined request removes its card). **Kind:** additive, a minor version. `tool-api.v1.4.schema.json` holds every v1.3 definition under its own name: the unchanged ones by `$ref` to `tool-api.v1.3.schema.json`, which stays as it is and refers to v1.2, v1.1 and v1; the changed ones (`stepKey`, `given`, `partResult`, `gateAndCommit.committed`, `gateAndCommit.response`, `gateAndCommit.exchange`, `callName`) take only new enum values. So every v1 to v1.3 exchange is a v1.4 exchange (`npm run contracts:check` checks every v1 to v1.3 fixture against v1.4). **Scope:** A6-01 to A6-32 and B6-01 to B6-35 (the 14 steps of each flow: A-J6.md, B-J6.md), with S12-33. K-14 v1 (the Stripe port), K-16 v1 (the calendar port), K-22 v1 (on call), K-29 v1 (consents, chat links), K-30 v1 (bookings and changes) and K-32 v1 (money), frozen on main, are named by table, column, call and event, never redefined.

The lead's notes for T-053 bind it: v1.4 only adds to v1.3; T-052's three rounds bind it (a K-02 choice field matches `^[a-z][a-z0-9_.]*$`; each default names its label as sent, its source and its walk, numbered after the last ones; no call charges on silence or holds money, and every charge names its bound yes, the K-29 consent its `effect.created` row's `approved_by` names, as v1.3 defaults 109 and 110 do; a card is kept only where K-32 says; a pay link reaches only the payer; C3's order (v1.3 default 104) and the deposit or full price charged at A6-10 or A6-03 (v1.3 default 87) are what v1.4 builds on; a 'no' in chat has a choice decide can pick; rows that carry words name a master line, or an open gap with a default); Master Corrections C3, C9, C10 and section 8 win over older master lines (Rosie never writes first; an ID is confirmed once). Q-08 and Q-56 are the defaults in use: a ready text from another mobile takes the wrong-number path, so the confirmation's chat link opens only the booking's own mobile's chat (Q-08, as v1 issueChatLink holds it); and the calendar double stands in for Google's and Microsoft's calendars, so the fresh read and the calendar writes run on it (Q-56; K-16 v1 "The double").

### The calls v1.4 adds

Every call reads PostgreSQL's **primary, uncached**, runs under v1.1's `businessCaller` (row-level security on every K-04, K-29, K-30 and K-32 row: arch 10 line 546; K-30 rule 5), names its person on the server, never from the browser's or an AI's words (K-22 v1 rule 4), takes `caller.requestId` as the key a repeat is known by, and can also answer `transient`. In a turn each write rides `gateAndCommit` as the chosen choice's effect, or as the next step of its walk, checked by limits 1 to 6; a call made alone commits under `caller.requestId`, waits for K-05's commit with no turn (CQ-44) and writes no outbox row. Callers are held by the schema (`dependentSchemas`).

| Call              | Gives (steps)                                                                      | Callers                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Writes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `askOwnerFirst`   | none (A6-02 to A6-07, A6-17; B6-04 to B6-09, B6-29)                                | ask: the customer's page or turn; ownerAnswer, offerTime, sendOffer: the owner's or a permitted admin's turn, or Needs you; customerAnswer: the customer's turn                                                                   | ask: K-30 owner_oks about request, its question to each answerer and its 30-minute reminder clock (default 113); ownerAnswer: owner_oks answered in K-08's ownerYes shape, then the walk (accept), A6-04's question (another time) or the decline's end (default 125); offerTime and sendOffer: A6-04's read-back and A6-17's question (default 116); customerAnswer: take walks on, A6-03's take writes its ticked_terms consent (default 117), no thanks ends it |
| `sendToOnCall`    | none (B6-16)                                                                       | ask: the customer's page or turn, or the owner's accept; proAnswer: the pro's own turn                                                                                                                                            | ask: the offer to whoever is on call (K-22 on_call), visit_now, 5 minutes, with no name or address; proAnswer: take walks to B6-10, a 'Can't' ends it declined and tells Alex and Sam at once (default 118)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `writeBooking`    | booked (A6-10, B6-10, A6-12; A6-32's, B6-35's 'Paid in time'), code (A6-14, B6-13) | write: the customer's page or turn, the owner's or the pro's turn, Needs you, the customer's own AI on D104's door, C-101's job after C3's link was paid; paid, chargeFailed: C-101's job; noneWorks: the customer's page or turn | the fresh read, then booked (the time, the head, the visits with their codes, the terms snapshot, calendar writes, the confirmation, the job row) or charging (the time, the snapshot, the charge with its bound yes) or takenMomentAgo (A6-11's next times); paid: booked; chargeFailed: A6-32's pay link or the page's own clock; noneWorks: A6-13 (defaults 111, 119, 121, 122, 127)                                                                            |
| `recordStaffTold` | staff_told (A6-15, B6-14)                                                          | the outbox dispatcher's job                                                                                                                                                                                                       | K-30 booking_details.staff_told_at, set once, when the pro's job row is first delivered (default 120)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `askAboutClash`   | none (S12-33)                                                                      | ask: C-136's job; answer: the owner's or a permitted admin's turn, or Needs you                                                                                                                                                   | ask: S12-33's question by a clock row due at once, its 8:00am deadline the day before, no title; answer: keep changes nothing, move walks to v1.5's owner move (default 124)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

No source gives these calls a time budget: in a turn each runs inside `gateAndCommit`'s 300 ms (arch 04 line 223); alone none (open gap 11). The fresh free/busy read is K-16's own (`freeBusy`), before the commit. New errors: `requestEnded`, `calendarUnreadable`, `notPaidYet` and `priceDiffers`. Shared shapes added: `messageWritten` (each row a commit writes, with the step whose line it carries and its send class), `journeySixMoneyWord` (v1.3's moneyWord with pay.staff and pay.after), `visitWritten` (a visit, never its code) and `chargeBound` (a payment with its bound yes).

**Booked only once what is due now is paid.** A6-10 'saves the booking. Anything due now is charged as it's written' and 'The confirmation waits until it's paid'; S13-55, the home step, puts the time first: 'What's due now is taken once the booking is written, so a time lost in that moment costs nothing'. So `writeBooking` write confirms the time first and then, with nothing due on a card, books it in the same commit; with a deposit or the full price due on the saved card, it writes the charge and books nothing until C-101's job sees it paid (`paid`), since a visit's check-in code is written once with its visit and never updated (K-30 rule 6) and must not go out before the money (A6-32: 'Never: confirm, send the code or give the pro the job before it's paid'). Nothing is ever held (RULES 3.2), no charge is made on silence, and every charge names its bound yes (default 127).

### Each key's one call (02-contracts section 8)

| Key        | Given by (A-J6.md, B-J6.md)                                        | Its home (02-contracts section 8)                                                                                                           | The one call      |
| ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- |
| booked     | A6-10, B6-10; A6-12 at a new time; A6-32's, B6-35's 'Paid in time' | K-30: booking status booked, the K-04 head's stage booked, set once what is due now is paid (default 111)                                   | `writeBooking`    |
| code       | A6-14, B6-13                                                       | K-30 booking_visits.check_in_code (FS booking.check_in_code), new each visit, sealed under its person's key, written with the visit         | `writeBooking`    |
| staff_told | A6-15, B6-14                                                       | K-30 booking_details.staff_told_at: 'when the pro's message was delivered (a field under Q-33)', set once by the dispatcher's delivery mark | `recordStaffTold` |

Each key is written in journey 6 by its one call alone, whose request description names it with its step ids, and whose response answers the keys it wrote in `gives`: `writeBooking`'s booked answer gives booked and, for every visit with a code, code (A6-10 then A6-14, B6-10 then B6-13, in one commit: `J6_CALL_PASSES`; a visit with nobody home has no code, B4-105, and gives booked alone); its other answers give none. `recordStaffTold` gives staff_told. `askOwnerFirst`, `sendToOnCall` and `askAboutClash` give none, and no v1 to v1.3 call gives one. In a turn the owner's '1 Accept', the customer's '1 Take it', a pick of the next times and the on-call pro's '1 Take it' walk on to the booking write in the same commit, so the part gives the keys of the steps its walk passes (`J6_WALK_PASSES`). The named rule `givesJourneySixKeys` checks every fixture that states a journey 6 step (below).

Expert review is the same request (K-01's expert is cut: K-30 approval) |

### The choices decide picks in journey 6's turns

No journey 6 answer in a turn carries a field but A6-04's typed time, whose fields' names K-01's answerField pattern admits (`^[a-z][a-z0-9_.]*$`; T-051 round 4). Each 'no' is a choice decide can pick (v1.3 default 107's lesson). Where the bundle holds the step's split, the options are its branches, label word for word; where it does not, they are option choices (v1.2 default 86's way), each with its label as sent, its definition and its walk:

| The question (step, twin)                 | Options as sent                                                                                           | `definition`                                                                                         | Source                                                | The walk (code's)                                                                                                            | `fields`                     |
| ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| A6-02 (B6-04): Sam replies?               | '1 Accept', '2 Another time', '3 Decline' (the split; 'No answer in time' its clock)                      | the split's Means lines ('Means 2 Another time: Sam offers another time, read back first')           | the bundle's split (A-J6.md, B-J6.md)                 | `askOwnerFirst` ownerAnswer accept, anotherTime or decline                                                                   | none                         |
| A6-04 (B6-06): 'What time can you offer?' | none: a typed answer                                                                                      | 'the other time, or another person, Sam offers instead'                                              | A6-04 (MASTER-FLOW-A lines 3194 to 3204); default 116 | `askOwnerFirst` offerTime: code re-parses the fields under limit 6 and reads them back                                       | offered_time, offered_person |
| A6-04 (B6-06): the read-back              | '1 Send it', '2 Type another time'                                                                        | 'yes, send this other time to the customer, word for word'; 'no, type another time'                  | A6-04's line; default 116                             | `askOwnerFirst` sendOffer sendIt (A6-17's question) or typeAnother ('What time can you offer?' again)                        | none                         |
| A6-17 (B6-29): does Alex take it?         | '1 Take it', '2 No thanks' (the split's '2 No thanks, or no reply')                                       | the split's Means lines ('they take it, in any words, in their own chat')                            | the bundle's split                                    | `askOwnerFirst` customerAnswer take (then `writeBooking` write) or noThanks                                                  | none                         |
| A6-03 (B6-05): today's terms, a fresh yes | '1 Take it', '2 No thanks' (A6-17's own words for a change the customer OKs within 2 of their text hours) | 'a fresh yes to today's terms, where the booking can keep a fee'; 'no: nothing is booked or charged' | A6-03 (MASTER-FLOW-A lines 3186 to 3189); default 117 | `askOwnerFirst` customerAnswer step A6-03: take writes the ticked_terms consent, then `writeBooking` write; noThanks ends it | none                         |
| A6-11 (B6-11): Alex picks?                | 'A' and 'B' (each a time, as sent), 'C Neither works' (the split's 'A or B', 'C, or no reply')            | the split's Means lines ('one of the next times, in any words, within 10 minutes')                   | the bundle's split                                    | `writeBooking` write at A6-12 with the pick, or noneWorks                                                                    | none                         |
| B6-16: does Jordan take it in 5 minutes?  | '1 Take it', '2 Can't' (the split's '2 Can't, or no reply')                                               | the split's Means lines ('A different day or a set time is a Can't')                                 | the bundle's split                                    | `sendToOnCall` proAnswer take (then `writeBooking` write) or cant                                                            | none                         |
| S12-33: a clash on the calendar           | '1 Keep the visit', '2 Move the visit'                                                                    | 'the visit stays as booked'; 'Sam moves the visit'                                                   | S12-33's line (MASTER-FLOW-A line 6576); default 124  | `askAboutClash` answer keep (nothing changes) or move (journey 7's owner move, K-09 v1.5)                                    | none                         |

Their `stakes` follow K-02: the money-moving and booking answers (A6-02's accept, A6-17's and A6-03's take, A6-11's pick, B6-16's take) are high; an option choice with no bundle branch carries the one reason `unknown`, as v1.2 default 86's do.

### Named rule added (`tool-api.rules.mjs`)

The eight earlier rules stay as they are; v1.4 adds one under a new name, with its tables exported beside it.

- **`givesJourneySixKeys`:** an exchange whose `given.stated` names a journey 6 step states exactly the keys that step gives (`J6_STEP_GIVES`, from A-J6.md and B-J6.md) with those of the steps its own commit passes (`J6_CALL_PASSES`: A6-10 then A6-14; A6-12 and A6-32 give A6-10's booked and A6-14's code), less code when no visit it wrote has one, and its outcome wrote exactly those keys; made alone, its call is the one call that writes each key (`J6_KEY_CALL`); in a turn (`gateAndCommit`) a part gives its step's own keys and may give those of the steps its walk passes (`J6_WALK_PASSES`).

The rules file's name carries no version, so the frozen-contract guard does not protect it (STATE note 25's class): QA's contract test (T-120) pins it.

### Producers and consumers of v1.4

- **Producer:** action-layer engineer: C-074 Tool API, with C-099 bookings (the booking written, the visits and their codes, the snapshot, owner_oks, the request's end), C-100 reservations (the time confirmed, reserved again, released), C-101 payments and ledger (the charge as the booking is written, A6-32's pay link, the job on a charge's result), C-133 the fresh read and C-138 calendar writes (K-16), C-103 chat link (the confirmation's), C-094 identity and access (who answers for the owner, who is on call), C-111 own-AI door, C-110 Agent API (the booking number, never the code), C-087 one commit per turn, C-075 frontier builder (the option choices), C-088 slot filler (the money words' rows, visit.code to its person), as journey 6's tasks land.
- **Consumers:** runtime engineer: C-046 handle actor, C-054 clock rows and C-055 clock arming (the owner's deadline and reminder, A6-17's and A6-11's clocks, B6-16's 5 minutes, A6-32's deadline, the page's own clock, S12-33's deadline), C-124 outbox dispatcher (`recordStaffTold` on the pro's delivery; each row's send class), C-044 gateway (K-14's and K-16's events, through K-05 v1.1, unchanged); AI engineer: C-114 context bundle, C-115 and C-116 decide (the choices above), C-118 composer (each row's master line, the money words, visit.code); frontend engineer, through K-21's journey 6 version (T-063): C-197 booking page, C-199 secure page, C-200 pay page and pay links, C-192 owner console (Needs you's answers), C-195 staff and job page; action-layer engineer: C-110 Agent API; architect: C-186 Tool API mock; QA: the contract test `tests/contract/tool-api.v1.4.test.ts` (T-120), C-140 eval harness, C-147 scenario tests.
- **Frozen before it, read here:** K-09 v1.3 (T-052), K-14 v1 (T-023), K-16 v1 (T-025), K-30 v1 (T-039), K-32 v1 (T-041), with v1.3's own (K-01 to K-05, K-07, K-08, K-17, K-22 to K-29, K-34 to K-37).
- **Not here:** QA's contract test `tool-api.v1.4.test.ts` (T-120); journey 6's K-21 screens (T-063); building the calls (the action layer's journey 6 tasks).

### Fixtures, v1.4 (in `fixtures/index.json` with version v1.4)

26 accepted and 13 refused, each refused for its one fault (with that fault fixed it is accepted: checked 2026-11-09).

- **The request the owner OKs, flow A** (after v1.3's `j5-card-saved-yes-page.json`): `j6-ask-owner-page.json` (A6-02 from the secure page), `j6-owner-accept-turn.json` (Sam's '1 Accept'), `j6-write-booking-after-ok-turn.json` (A6-03 and A6-10 in Sam's turn: booked, code), `j6-staff-told-job.json` (A6-15 delivered: staff_told).
- **Money due as the booking is written:** `j6-write-booking-charging-page.json` (the Groom's package's $40 deposit, charging, after v1.3's `j5-deposit-yes-page.json`), `j6-booking-paid-job.json` (paid: booked, code), `j6-charge-failed-pay-link-job.json` (A6-32 after an OK: one pay link), `j6-pay-link-paid-job.json` (A6-32's 'Paid in time': booked, code), `j6-charge-failed-now-job.json` (a booking made now: 'Try another card' and the page's clock).
- **The time taken a moment ago:** `j6-taken-moment-ago-page.json` (A6-11's next times), `j6-pick-next-time-page.json` (A6-12: booked, code), `j6-none-works-turn.json` (A6-13).
- **The owner's other time and decline:** `j6-owner-another-time-turn.json`, `j6-owner-offer-read-back-turn.json`, `j6-owner-offer-sent-turn.json` (A6-04 to A6-17), `j6-customer-take-turn.json`, `j6-customer-no-thanks-turn.json` (A6-17's answers), `j6-owner-decline-page.json` (A6-05 from Needs you).
- **Sent now, flow B:** `j6-on-call-ask-page.json` (B6-16), `j6-on-call-take-turn.json`, `j6-write-booking-sent-now-turn.json` (B6-10: booked, code), `j6-on-call-cant-turn.json`.
- **The AI's doors:** `j6-write-booking-agent-d104.json` (D104: booked at once on the AI's request, the booking number and chat link to the AI, never the code) and `j6-write-booking-c3-paid-job.json` (C3's booking write once the pay link was paid, after v1.3's pay-page fixtures).
- **S12-33:** `j6-clash-ask-job.json` and `j6-clash-keep-turn.json`.
- **Refused:** `invalid/refuse-j6-code-to-agent.json` (the check-in code in an AI's answer), `invalid/refuse-j6-booked-while-charging.json` (booked before the charge is paid), `invalid/refuse-j6-charge-without-consent.json` (a charge with no bound yes), `invalid/refuse-j6-owner-accept-on-silence.json` (the deadline sent as the owner's answer), `invalid/refuse-j6-owner-answer-from-agent.json` (an AI answering for the owner), `invalid/refuse-j6-staff-told-from-page.json` (staff_told from a page), `invalid/refuse-j6-clash-title-shown.json` (a personal event's title), `invalid/refuse-j6-paid-from-page.json` (a page saying it is paid), `invalid/refuse-j6-pay-link-url-to-ai.json` (A6-32's link for the caller to hand on), `invalid/refuse-j6-taken-but-charged.json` (a charge when the time was lost), `invalid/refuse-j6-on-call-offer-routine.json` (B6-16's offer held for text hours) and, by the rule `givesJourneySixKeys`, `invalid/refuse-j6-staff-told-stated-as-code.json` (the pro's delivery stated as A6-14) and `invalid/refuse-j6-booked-without-code.json` (a booking with a code giving booked alone).

Names and values come from the master's sample cast and fixture set v1's demo shops (Alex M., Sam, Riley, Dee W., Ben T., Jordan; Lee's Barbershop's $35 haircut with its $20 fee, the $140 Groom's package with its $40 deposit, the style consult, the $55 Signature cut and hot towel shave; Lee Home Services' kitchen drain clearing with its $89 call and Jordan on call weeknights, K-22's seed-on-call; A6-02's, A6-04's, A6-11's, A6-17's, B6-16's and S12-33's own sample times and lines; K-14's charge-failed-pay-link.json and decline-not-retried.json stories; October and November 2026), never a real person (rule 9). Ids reuse K-14's, K-30's, K-32's and v1.3's where the rows are theirs (`booking-agent-alex-oct21`, `consent-agent-alex-haircut-terms`, `card-agent-alex-haircut`, `line-agent-alex-1`, `booking-alex-grooms-package`, `consent-alex-grooms-terms`, `pay-alex-grooms-deposit`, `eff-alex-grooms-deposit`, `rsv-alex-grooms-package`, `eff-alex-color-correction-pay-link`, `booking-agent-dee-style-consult`, `booking-agent-dee-signature-cut`, `pay-agent-dee-signature-cut`, `consent-dee-signature-cut-terms`, `staff-jordan-home-services`, `callink-riley-google`). These are examples, not from the sources (v1.3 default 48's rule): every other booking, visit, reservation, owner OK, open question, outbox row, clock row, effect, consent, pay link, payment, chat link and busy block id, the booking numbers, the chat link's placeholder code, the length of a haircut (30 minutes) and the times of day the steps do not print.

### Defaults used (T-053)

111. **New, when the booking is written and when it is booked (A6-10; S13-55, the home step; A6-32; K-30 v1 rule 6):** `writeBooking` write first reads the calendars once more (K-16 freeBusy, purpose bookingWrite: arch 05 line 259; a failed read is A6-10's safe default, `calendarUnreadable`), then confirms the time (K-05 v1 commitTurn confirms the hold booked; a hold that lapsed under K-05 v1's 10 minutes is reserved again if the time is still free: 'An answer after the deadline: check the time is still free', A6-02; v1.3 default 98), all in one commit. Nothing due now on a card (nothing taken, a card saved only, a line paid by credit, C3's pay link already paid): the head booked, each visit with its new check-in code, the terms snapshot naming the money step's yes (K-30 booking_terms.terms_consent_id: the money step's ticked_terms consent, never a pack's, v1.3 default 109; null for a nothing-taken yes), the calendar writes, A6-14's confirmation and A6-15's job row: booked, giving booked and code. A deposit or the full price due now on the saved card: the time confirmed and the snapshot written, the K-32 payment pending with its one stripe_charge (customer present only from the customer's own page), bound to the money step's yes (default 127), and nothing else: no visit, no code, no message and no key, since 'The confirmation waits until it's paid' and a visit's code is written once with its visit and never updated (K-30 rule 6: booking_visits takes no update of check_in_code). The charge's result `charged`, or paymentSucceeded for a charge that needed the bank's check, makes it paid (the runtime side: K-32 rule 5), and C-101's handling of it calls `writeBooking` paid, whose commit books it as above through clock rows due at once (a job's commit writes no outbox row). booked is K-04's head stage booked (02-contracts section 8: 'K-30: booking status booked'), set at most once a booking is paid or owes nothing now.
112. **New, how each row reaches its reader (`messageWritten`; MC C2, C9; RULES 4.3, 4.8; K-07 v1 sendClass):** in a turn an outbox row of that commit, to whichever reader the step names (a turn may write rows to other recipients, as v1.3's ownerAnswer tells the customer); from a page's, an agent's or a job's commit a clock row due at once on the reader's handle, whose clock turn writes the row (v1.2 default 72's way). A customer's row goes into their open chat, else to their email, never first into a chat Rosie would open (MC C9). Send classes: visit_now for a job sent now's rows (B6-04's 5 minutes, B6-16's offer and its 'Can't', B6-13's confirmation and B6-14's job row: 'Urgent texts go at once, even at night'; 'the pro who took it is told at once'); quick_reply for the reply in the same turn to the reader's own text (Q-15); routine for every other row, which limit 8 holds to text hours (the owner's question and reminder: 'The clock runs only in Sam's text hours'; A6-17's question; the confirmation and the job row of a set time; A6-32's link; S12-33's question). Each names the step whose line it carries, its master text (K-03 composeInput; default 123 for a reader the step prints no line for).
113. **New, the owner's ask (A6-01, A6-02, B6-04; K-30 v1 owner_oks; K-22 v1 who OKs what):** from the stored approval ownerok, after the customer's yes (A5-31's 'Done: the yes'), `askOwnerFirst` ask writes one owner_oks row about request, answer_by 4 of the owner's text hours ('No answer in 4 of Sam's text hours: not booked'; the plug-in's shorter clock where it sets one, always before the visit), one question per answerer, the owner and each admin the owner lets answer ('The first answer from Sam or Pat counts': the first answer closes the others, withdrawn), each A6-02's line with its open question and the split's options, and one clock row 30 minutes later in the owner's text hours, whose firing, while unanswered, writes A6-02's reminder line to the owner and its wait line to the customer ('No answer in 30 minutes: the customer is told to wait, and Sam gets one reminder') and sets owner_oks.reminded_at. 'Nothing is charged for this visit until the OK' and 'Never: take money or confirm before Sam's yes': no payment and no effect. The time stays saved only as K-05 v1 allows (open gap 109). The owner's answer is the owner's own or a permitted admin's, in their own turn or on Needs you, in K-08's ownerYes shape, never an AI's.
114. **New, the choices of journey 6 (K-02 v1 optionOffered; arch 03 lines 183, 187; v1.3 default 107's lesson):** where the bundle holds the step's split (A6-02, A6-17, A6-11, B6-16), decide picks its branches, label word for word, each 'no' a branch of its own ('3 Decline', '2 No thanks, or no reply', 'C, or no reply', '2 Can't, or no reply'), and the silent branch ('No answer in time', 'or no reply') is the question clock's firing, never a choice decide picks; where it does not (A6-04's read-back, A6-03's fresh yes, S12-33's question: open gap 107), the options are option choices (v1.2 default 86's way), each with its label as sent, its definition and its walk (table "The choices decide picks in journey 6's turns"). No answer carries a field but A6-04's typed time (default 116).
115. **New, a job sent now the owner OKs (B6-04's and B6-09's vary; B6-03):** answer_by is 5 minutes at any hour ('That clock is 5 minutes, any hour'), with no 30-minute reminder; '2 Another time' is a decline ('On a job sent now, 2 Another time is a decline: Alex is offered the soonest set times'), written as owner_oks another_time and ended declined, with B6-07's line and its link to pick another time; an accept walks to B6-16 ('Once he accepts, B6-16 texts the on-call pro'); no answer in 5 minutes is the clock's, 'Nobody is sent, and nothing is taken', with B6-09's sent-now line.
116. **New, the owner's other time (A6-04, B6-06; arch 06 line 309; v1.2 default 80):** '2 Another time' writes 'What time can you offer?' with its open question, which takes a typed answer; decide gives it as the fields offered_time (the owner's words for the time) and offered_person (the words for another person, or none: the same person stays), names K-01's answerField pattern admits, and code re-parses them under limit 6 into the time and a person of the business (`offerTime`). The read-back, A6-04's line word for word, holds the other time in its stored words (v1.2 default 80's form; open gap 87), with the option choices '1 Send it' ('yes, send this other time to the customer, word for word') and '2 Type another time' ('no, type another time'); '1 Send it' writes A6-17's question from those words ('Safe default: nothing goes to the customer until Sam confirms the read-back'). The other time is not held (K-05 v1 holds 10 minutes at most: open gap 109), so the customer's '1 Take it' books it only if it is still free ('A late yes after that: check the time is still free'), and it is offered only at the line's price (default 122).
117. **New, A6-03's fresh yes to today's terms (A6-03, B6-05: 'A request made before free cancel ended, with the yes after it? The customer first sees today's terms, where the booking can keep a fee. Nothing is charged without their fresh yes to them. No yes: nothing is charged. No yes in 2 of their text hours, ending before the visit: not booked'; A6-02):** when the owner's accept comes after the visit's free-cancel time and the booking keeps a fee (its lines' fee_cents or deposit), the accept's walk writes no booking: it asks the customer, in their chat or by email (MC C9), a question whose words are the money step's own words rebuilt by code from the rows read with today's free-cancel time and fee (v1.1 default 52's rule; v1.3 default 108's way; open gap 107), its options A6-17's own '1 Take it' and '2 No thanks', the words A6-02's family gives a change the customer OKs within 2 of their text hours, and its clock 2 of their text hours ending before the visit. '1 Take it' (`askOwnerFirst` customerAnswer, step A6-03) writes the fresh yes as a K-29 ticked_terms consent with those exact words and their sha256 (`consentId`), which the terms snapshot and the charge's approved_by then name, and walks to `writeBooking` write; '2 No thanks' or no reply: not booked, nothing charged, the card removed (default 125). A time or person change at the same price and terms keeps the money step's yes, so A6-17's and A6-11's answers write no consent.
118. **New, the on-call pro (B6-16; K-22 v1 on_call; S12-17):** whoever is on call for the booking's kind of work now (K-22 on_call: its days, hours and staff) gets one offer, B6-16's line, visit_now, its clock 5 minutes; until Take it, 'the text shows only the kind of job and the area' (with the sensitive switch 'only times and initials'), and the name and address open in the job after, or once it's charged where money is due now. Nobody on call is B6-16's 'Can't, or no reply' at once. The pro's answer stands for the owner's ('Work sent now: the on-call pro's yes stands for Sam's': S12-17), so a 'Can't' ends the request declined and no reply in 5 minutes no_answer (K-30's closest whys: open gap 106), the card removed, and Alex and Sam told at once with B6-16's own lines, Alex's with the soonest times' link (v1.2 linkIssued bookingPage). A take with a later arrival is no plain take (open gap 117).
119. **New, the confirmation and its code (A6-14, B6-13; MC C2, C9; Q-03, Q-08; K-30 rule 7; K-03 visit.code):** A6-14's confirmation goes to the person the code is for (the person served, or whoever meets the pro: K-30 code_for_person_id), into their open chat, else by email, and on the page; the code is new for each visit, sealed under that person's key, and never in any answer: only the slot filler puts it into that person's confirmation (K-03 visit.code, audience customer). The customer's own AI gets the booking number (K-30 booking_details.number) and the booking's chat link, never the code, a street address, a card or a private detail (A6-14; FS line 675). The chat link (K-29 chat_links, its ready text and short code) is issued for each new booking whose mobile's chat is not open and whose mobile is a Linq contact (Q-03; MC C9: 'each new booking's confirmation carries the link again'), its QR beside it on the page and in the email, never in a text, its url to a page or an AI once; a ready text from another mobile takes the wrong-number path (Q-08).
120. **New, the pro's job and staff_told (A6-15, B6-14; 02-contracts section 8; K-30 v1 README):** the booking's commit writes A6-15's job row to the visit's staff member (K-22), with the job, the time and {{pay.staff}}, never the code, answers or notes ('Answers and notes: open the job'; a child as 'Alex M.'s child'); when that row's first part is delivered (K-05 v1 markPart delivered, firstDelivery), the outbox dispatcher (C-124) calls `recordStaffTold`, which sets booking_details.staff_told_at once and gives staff_told. A row never delivered gives none; its next way is v1's `sendsFailing`. The job page's link has no K-03 slot (open gap 108).
121. **New, a failed charge (A6-10's 'The charge fails', A6-32, B6-35; K-14 v1 'Pay links', decline-not-retried.json, charge-failed-pay-link.json; RULES 3.11):** a decline is never retried. On a booking made now, with the customer on the page, 'it shows on the page, as any declined card': `writeBooking` chargeFailed arms the page's own clock (A5-31) at the hold's held_until, whose firing lets the time go and ends the request let_go if nothing is paid by then (A5-06), and the page offers 'Try another card' (S12-25), then v1.3's card stage and a new write with a new charge on its own key. After an OK, and on a job sent now after one: one pay link, a new K-32 payment pending with its stripe_pay_link (saveCardForLater where the booking keeps a card: 'that card is the one saved for it'), its pay_links row due by {{clock.pay}}, the request's latest answer time ('The time stays saved until then'), carried by clock rows due at once only to the payer's own ways, their open chat and their email, never an AI, the owner or a pro; on a job sent now the pro hears at once 'Payment didn't go through yet. Don't set out.' (B6-35's own words, default 123). Paid in time: `writeBooking` paid books it, and the declined card is removed (nothing_needs_it). Not paid: the pay link's clock firing (closePayLink) ends the request not_paid, lets the time go and removes the card, and anyone told hears at once ('Called off: not paid. Nothing to do.').
122. **New, a new time keeps the line's price (A6-11, A6-17: 'The new price shows before any yes'; K-30 v1 rule 6, the line's copy never changes):** A6-11's next times and A6-04's other time are offered only where their exact price for that time and person is the line's copied price, so the money the customer said yes to stands and no fresh yes is needed (`priceDiffers` otherwise). This fails closed and offers fewer times than the master's: a time at another price waits for open gap 110.
123. **New, the words of rows the step prints no line for (K-03 v1 composeInput masterText; v1.3 default 108's way):** where a step names a reader's notice but prints no line for it, the row's master text is the step's own words for it, word for word where it quotes them: A6-17's end ('The request is dropped and nothing is booked. The customer and Sam are told.', with 'A no thanks drops the request: {{pay.back}}' for the customer); B6-35's pro ('Payment didn't go through yet. Don't set out.'; 'Called off: not paid. Nothing to do.'); S12-33's pro on no answer ('the visit stays as booked'); A6-03's fresh-yes question (default 117). C-118's plan keeps those words word for word, as v1.2 default 80's read-back is kept, and every money word names its row (v1.3 default 90). Open gap 107.
124. **New, a personal event over a visit (S12-11, S12-33; K-16 v1 readChanges, busy blocks with no title):** when C-136's read of a calendar change finds a busy block of a booked person's own calendar over a booked visit of theirs, never one of Rosie's own events, its job calls `askAboutClash` ask: S12-33's line to the owner by a clock row due at once, routine, its open question with the option choices '1 Keep the visit' ('the visit stays as booked') and '2 Move the visit' ('Sam moves the visit'), its clock 8:00am the day before the visit in the business's zone. No title is ever shown ('It may be private'), and the customer hears nothing unless the visit changes. Keep changes nothing; move walks to journey 7's owner move (K-09 v1.5), and until then the visit stays (S12-33's safe default; open gap 116); no answer: the visit stays and the pro is told (default 123). The question has no owner_oks row (open gap 106).
125. **New, a request that ends unbooked (A6-05, A6-07, A6-13, A6-17, B6-16; S13-55; K-30 v1 default 7; v1.3 default 103):** the commit that ends it sets request_ended_why (declined, no_answer, dropped, let_go or not_paid), the head cancelled (K-30 default 7), the hold let go where one is still held (K-05 v1 release, let_go), a card saved for it removed at once ('A booking that's declined or never made removes it at once': removed_why not_booked; K-14 removeCard by the deciding turn or job, K-14 open gap 2), a pack bought on the page stays bought, and {{pay.back}} reads the record (what was removed or refunded), never a key; nothing was charged, so nothing is refunded.
126. **New, the AI's doors at the booking write (MC C3; v1.3 defaults 88, 91, 104):** on D104's door (nothing to pay or save, approval now, the hold held for the AI's agent token) the customer's own AI calls `writeBooking` write itself, decided from those stored facts, and gets the booking number and the chat link, never the code; on C3's door the booking is written once K-14's paymentSucceeded has made the pay link's payment paid, by C-101's job (v1.3 default 104's step 6), with nothing due on a card. An AI's write is never answered charging.
127. **New, every charge names its bound yes (the lead's note on T-053; v1.3 defaults 109, 110; K-32 v1 rule 10; K-08 v1 approvalConsentRow):** each stripe_charge and stripe_pay_link journey 6 writes (`chargeBound`) has its `effect.created` event-log row's approved_by name the K-29 consent behind it, the same one the booking's terms snapshot names: the money step's ticked_terms consent (v1.3 takeMoneyBefore yes), or A6-03's fresh yes's (default 117); the owner's accept is his own answer in owner_oks (K-08 ownerYes), never the customer's yes. So C-018's hourly check finds each journey 6 charge's yes by either road, and a charge with none is an unbound charge, refunded at once (K-32 rule 10).

### Open gaps (v1.4)

For QUESTIONS.md, each with its default where one is in use:

106. **No home for two journey 6 facts** (defaults 118, 124): K-30 v1 owner_oks.about has no value for S12-33's clash question, so it lives only in its K-04 open question; and request_ended_why has none for 'nobody on call took it' (B6-16), so the pro's 'Can't' is written declined and his silence no_answer, as the pro's answer stands for the owner's (S12-17). Proposed: a K-30 minor version (about clash; why no_pro). The architect's next K-30 task.
107. **Questions the bundle holds no split or line for** (defaults 114, 116, 117, 123, 124): A6-04's read-back and S12-33's question print their options in their lines but have no split, and A6-03's fresh yes has neither a question nor a line; B6-35's pro lines and A6-17's end notices are quoted in the steps' words only. Proposed: the K-01 minor version of v1.2 open gap 88 and v1.3 open gap 94 gives them splits and lines, their words through the compiler (C-058) or an overlay Jay approves (C-071), the playbook engineer's; then defaults 117 and 123 are retired.
108. **No K-03 slot for the job page's link** (default 120): A6-15 and B6-14 end 'Open the job: rosfield.com/j/a1', and K-03 v1 has no slot for it (v1.2 open gap 70's class). Default: the job row carries no link until a K-03 minor version adds link.job; the pro opens the job on the signed-in staff page (K-21, T-063). For the lead and the architect's next K-03 task.
109. **K-05's longer holds, for journey 6** (defaults 111, 113, 116; v1.3 open gap 93): K-05 v1 holds a saved time 10 minutes, so the owner's 4 text hours (A6-02), the customer's 2 text hours (A6-17; A6-11 after an OK), A6-32's 'The time stays saved until then' and A5-31's page clock after a decline outlast it, and no call moves a booked reservation back to held. Default: the time is checked again at the write and reserved again if still free; a time confirmed for a charge stays the booking's until the charge's result, the page's clock or the pay link's deadline lets it go. Proposed: the K-05 minor version of v1.3 open gap 93.
110. **A new price at a new time** (default 122): 'The new price shows before any yes' (A6-11, A6-17), but K-30 v1 never changes a line's copy and no frozen field holds a fresh yes to a new price. Proposed: a K-30 minor version for a line copied again at the customer's yes to a new price, and the K-09 minor version that takes it; until then such times are not offered.
111. **CQ-44, restated for journey 6:** every call made alone (the pages', the AI's, C-101's, C-136's and the dispatcher's jobs) waits for K-05's commit with no turn (open gap 3).
112. **The K-05 reads journey 6 needs:** a booking's hold and its state, its lines with their paths, the saved card, the owner OK by id, the open question's stored options and words (A6-04's read-back, A6-11's times), the visit's staff, who is on call now, each booked person's calendar link, a payment's state and its effect, and the booking's chat link and mobile. A K-05 minor version adds them before the action layer's journey 6 tasks; until then those tasks wait (NEED), with no default read.
113. **K-08 kinds for journey 6's writes** (CQ-56): a booking written, a charge as it is written, a pay link after a decline, a visit's code, an owner's ask and answer, an offer sent, a request ended, staff told, a clash asked; CQ-56's default applies.
114. **K-21's journey 6 version (T-063):** how the booking page, the secure page and the pay page show the confirmation (the code to its person only), a decline and 'Try another card', the next times and their pick; how Needs you sends the owner's answers; the staff job page and what it opens when.
115. **A late answer after the deadline** (A6-02: 'An answer after the deadline: check the time is still free, then ask the customer again'): owner_oks.answer is set once, and no answer is written over no_answer. Default: a late answer is `requestEnded`, and nothing is booked; the customer was told at the deadline (A6-07). Proposed: a K-30 or K-09 minor version for a second ask of the customer.
116. **S12-33's '2 Move the visit'** (default 124): the owner's move is journey 7's (K-09 v1.5, 'the owner moves it'). Default: the answer is recorded and the visit stays as booked until v1.5. The architect's v1.5 task.
117. **B6-16's later arrival** (default 118): 'A later arrival, like I'll take it but I'm 2 hours out, is a new estimate, still sent now. Alex is asked Keep it?' has no branch, question or line. Default: such a take is no option of the offer, decide picks neither, and with no plain take in 5 minutes it is B6-16's 'Can't, or no reply'. Proposed: the K-01 minor version of open gap 107 gives it its branch and words.

## Change log

- v1 draft, 2026-11-07 (T-009): the turn face and the shared core; no fixtures, not frozen.
- v1, 2026-11-07 (T-018): frozen. Added the setup calls of journeys 1 and 2 (`matchKinds`, `lookupListing`, `saveSetupStep` with one variant per step, `readSetup`), `saveSetupStep` as a core action (S2-05's ready text), `partResult.gives`, the control route of `sendProofCode` and `proveCode` (S1-75), the staff invite's 48-hour clock, `<call>.exchange` and `given` for every call but `plannerVerbs`, the root `anyOf` of the exchanges, the named rules `statedOutcome` and `givesStepKeys`, 50 accepted and 2 refused fixtures with their index, this README's freeze record, and `frozen.json`.
- v1, round 2, 2026-11-07 (T-018, before the merge, so v1 itself changed): the eight points of the first verification. (1) The clock branch: `gateAndCommit` takes `input.kind` clock with a clock turn or none, answers `fired` with a nullable turn id, and has `notArmed`, `notDue` and the clock row's `versionChanged`; the rule `clockTurn` and two clock fixtures. (2) START only from the phone's own START text (`inboundEventId`; any other caller refused), and `notFromThatPhone`. (3) The core action `saveSetupStep` takes only S2-05's ready text (`saveSetupStepInTurn`). (4) Limit 6 after decide's re-read: `valueFailsWords` before any write, the rule `rereadBeforeQuestion`, the limit 6 fixtures. (5) `decideReviewItem` writes its decision back (K-23 control, K-24 new work) and answers what it wrote. (6) The owner's typed email: `sendProofCode` route typedEmail. (7) Limit 1's code and question in its outcome, and `proveCode`'s `redecideTurnId`. (8) S1-12 has no read-back: no `yesEventId`; the limit 3 fixture is S2-22's. Also the notes no later minor version could add (defaults 30 to 33, the planner refusal's wording), the security reviewer in the freeze record, 9 accepted and 5 refused fixtures more (59 and 7 in all).
- v1, round 3, 2026-11-07 (T-018, before the merge, rebased onto main with K-22, K-25 and K-28 v1, so v1 itself changed): the second verification's point and notes. `sendProofCode.forPersonId`, the person a code is for (K-22 v1 `proof_codes.person_id` and the account), set by the server, never the browser, with `caller.personId` null on a sign-in's and the owner email's send (`sendProofCode.request`'s `dependentSchemas` on `purpose`); `email-check-typed.json` and the sign-in sends name it, and `control-code-email.json` gives it null. K-09's proof-code purposes mapped to K-22 v1 ("Proof codes in K-22 v1"; defaults 27 and 34; open gaps 2 and 24). The S2-22 card in the limit 1, 3 and 4 fixtures in the master's words. A bare STOP in its own core-only turn (default 35). The timing table's work-phone clock as default 24's clock turn. Two refused fixtures more (59 accepted and 9 refused in all).
- v1.1, 2026-11-07 (T-050): frozen, a minor version that only adds. Why: 02-contracts K-09's version table, row v1.1 (journey 3, frozen at F15 before its first task), and T-246's open point (the handle actor's safety record on arrival). Added: `openRequest` (gives request), `confirmBusinessBooking` (gives who_for, reach_by, times and customer), `findCustomer`, `checkBookingFirst` and `setCustomerBlock` (S11-62); `recordSafetyOutcome` gives safe, takes the actor's record on arrival (`actorOnArrival`, the arrival turn) and returns the hold's version, and `releaseSafetyHold` takes the actor's at-once release; `partResult.gives` and `given.stated` may name journey 3's keys; the named rules `givesJourneyKeys` and `arrivalTurn`; 17 accepted and 8 refused fixtures. Consumers: the action-layer engineer (C-074, C-099, C-094, C-105, C-104, C-110), the runtime engineer (C-046, C-047; T-246's rework), the frontend engineer through K-21 (C-197, C-192, C-199; T-060), the AI engineer (C-114, C-115), the Tool API mock (C-186) and QA (T-117, C-140, C-147). No v1 file changed.
- v1.1, round 2, 2026-11-07 (T-050, before the merge, so v1.1 itself changed; v1 did not): the first verification's points. (1) The arrival turn's id is `arrivalTurnId`, the hash of the marked input `arrival:` and the event id, never a batch turn's; the rule `arrivalTurn` checks it and the batch turn after it (`j3-safety-arrival-batch-turn.json`); a timed-out read on arrival (`j3-safety-arrival-timeout.json`) and its outage line's send class (default 36; runtime, action-layer and security reviews). (2) The reply books it: '1 Yes' and '2 Not yet' confirm the held time booked and set the stage booked (default 43; action-layer FAIL 1). (3) The BOOK's values are taken and stored in the BOOK turn (`openRequest.entered`: the line, a new customer's handles, the time held by K-05 reserve), and the reply confirms what is stored, '3 Change it' (`changeIt`) included (defaults 43, 53; action-layer FAIL 3, AI FAIL 1). (4) `readBackBusinessBooking` gives the Today page its read-back's words and versions; in a turn the reply names its open question (default 52; frontend FAIL 2, security note c). (5) A listed customer's contacts come from the row (default 49; frontend FAIL 1). (6) v1's open gap 18: STOP tells each business with an open booking, and '1 Yes' on a stopped mobile writes no consent and tells only that texts are off (`recordStopOrStart.response.businessesTold`, `coreResult`, `confirmBusinessBooking.response.textsOff`; default 50; security FAIL 1). Also: `checkBookingFirst`'s per-contact limit (default 51), a typed number never revealed as known (default 49), the doors A3-51 and A3-43 (default 54), CQ-44 restated and the K-05 reads named (open gaps 43, 44), open gaps 41, 42, 45 and 46. 25 accepted and 10 refused v1.1 fixtures (8 accepted and 2 refused added; 17 changed). The response holds the booking too: '1 Yes' and '2 Not yet' answer stage booked with the reservation booked (`refuse-j3-not-yet-left-held.json`).
- v1.1, round 3, 2026-11-08 (T-050, before the merge, so v1.1 itself changed; v1 did not): the second verification's three points. (1) `openRequest` at door business writes `booking_details.taken` 'nothing' once in the BOOK's insert, so A3-30's and B3-08's '2 Not yet' can show; K-30's "Null until the money step" holds for the customer's own doors (default 55; `j3-confirm-business-booking-not-yet-text.json`; action-layer FAIL 1). (2) `confirmBusinessBooking.response.textsOff.outboxRowId` is null for a page caller, which shows the line itself, and `confirmBusinessBooking.exchange` holds it to the caller (null on the page, an id in a turn); no consent is written (`j3-confirm-business-booking-texts-off-page.json`; security FAIL 1, action-layer FAIL 2). (3) A STOP made with no turn arms one clock row per business, due at once on the owner's handle, whose clock turn writes the notice within the minute; `businessesTold[].outboxRowId` is null for it (default 56, open gap 43; `j3-stop-on-return-tells-businesses.json`; security FAIL 2). README lines only: line 304's exception for `recordSafetyOutcome` in a turn (action-layer note f); defaults 46, 52 and 53 and the open times' source (AI notes g, a, e, d); open gap 44's reads (action-layer note a); open gap 27's default and open gaps 47 to 53 (AI notes b, c, f; action-layer notes b to e, g). 28 accepted and 10 refused v1.1 fixtures (3 accepted added; none changed).
- v1.2, 2026-11-08 (T-051): frozen, a minor version that only adds. Why: 02-contracts K-09's version table, row v1.2 (journey 4, frozen at F16 before its first task), and v1.1's pointers to it. Added: `matchService` (gives service), `setWhoFor` (who_for), `setReachWay` (reach_by), `addCustomer` (customer), `setPrice` (price_known, visit_fee), `quoteFromPhotos` (quote), `showTimes` (times), `saveTime` (choice, time_saved), `joinWaitlist`, `sendSecureLink` (contact, link), `checkPlace` (place, address) and `saveSecurePage` (access, answers); `partResult.gives` and `given.stated` may name journey 4's keys (`journeyFourKey`); the named rule `givesJourneyFourKeys`; 20 accepted and 9 refused fixtures. - v1.2, round 2, 2026-11-08 (T-051, before the merge, so v1.2 itself changed; v1 and v1.1 did not): the first verification's points as the lead's nine rulings fold them (DL-162; section "v1.2", "Round 2"). (1) '2 Change it' carries the new price, stored as the pending change before its read-back, and `changeReadBack` applies it on the exact yes (default 80). (2) `linkIssued` for every link a call issues: a teen's parent link (`setWhoFor.response.parentLink`, default 73), see first's times link and askPhotos' photos link by its via (`quoteFromPhotos.response.link`, defaults 62, 79), the photos page (`saveSecurePage` photos), and the quote link with a page's message through a clock row (`setPrice`, defaults 71, 72), each naming its K-03 slot or open gap 70. (3) The meets-pro adult's own link (`sendSecureLink` for meetsPro, default 83) and their mobile, texts yes and meets_pro_person_id on it (`saveSecurePage` meetsProYes, default 85). (4) The saved time's door (`saveTime.response.reservation.door`; C3's 15 minutes only for an AI's instant money booking) and `extendTime` (default 61). (5) A4-80 by screen 2's categories with `servedAgeBand` (defaults 74, 76). (6) Both flows' ways with their prices, the place pick moving the request to the other flow, and `checkPlace` naming its way (default 75; v1.1 open gap 27 closed). (7) `addCustomer` carries the way and the band (default 77). (8) A bounded waitlist and a texts-off answer (defaults 81, 82; open gap 71). (9) An area check keeps only a town or ZIP match (`matchPrecision`, default 84). Cheap notes: the rule's turn branch (`J4_CALL_PASSES`, `J4_WALK_PASSES`, sentNow), answer-field names (default 76), `setReachWay`'s effects pinned to contacts/add, `savedTimeCap`, the four flow B fixtures' seed, askPhotos from a bound token. - v1.2, round 3, 2026-11-08 (T-051, before the merge, so v1.2 itself changed; v1 and v1.1 did not): the second verification's points as the lead's three rulings fold them (DL-171; section "v1.2", "Round 3"). (1) Who a saved time is held for, one rule per caller, the same in `saveTime`'s and `extendTime`'s descriptions, default 61 and the notes of `j4-save-time-agent.json` and `j4-save-time-turn.json`: an agent caller's save for its agent token (from its own authentication, never a field it fills; arch 05 line 247's cap counts it), a page's or a turn's for the booker's mobile on file (`saveTime.errors` `mobileRequired` with none); `extendTime` decides from that stored holder, never the caller's word: a mobile's hold is the standard door's and is extended (`extendTime.response.heldFor` phoneNumber), and every time held for an agent token is refused (`notExtendable`) until a K-05 or K-30 minor version records the hold's door (open gap 85), so `saveTime.exchange` holds an agent caller's standard save to extendable false and its lapse texts nobody. (2) `refuse-j4-meets-pro-link-texted.json` asked by text in Alex's own turn, refused by the meets-pro rule alone. (3) The shows of `j4-match-service-agent.json` and `j4-save-time-agent.json`.
